# 子 Agent · 你负责 ShopStack 项目的 **Flutter 移动端（`apps/flutter/`）** 从 24 页扩到 **80 页**。

> 第 1 / 2 卷

## 👤 用户 · 2026-10-20 14:34:45

你负责 ShopStack 项目的 **Flutter 移动端（`apps/flutter/`）** 从 24 页扩到 **80 页**。

## 项目背景

ShopStack 是一个中大型电商项目（Go 微服务后端 + Flutter 移动端 + 微信小程序 + Vue3 Web 用户端 + React 后台管理端）。
本轮任务是把**每一端**各自扩到 80 页。你负责 Flutter 移动端这一端。

工作目录：`/<WORKSPACE>/shopstack`

## 必读文档（先读这两份）

1. **`docs/page-expansion-plan.md`** —— 页面清单的**唯一来源**。里面有 Flutter 端需要新增的 56 个页面的完整清单
   （编号、页面名、dart 文件名、数据来源接口）。严格按清单实现，不要自行增删页面。
2. **`docs/frontend-worker-brief.md`** —— 通用规则：目录所有权、Mock 兜底、金额格式、接口调用规范、
   端口提示、设计令牌、验收命令。这份文件里的所有规则对你同样生效。

## 你的唯一所有权范围

- 你**只能**修改 `apps/flutter/**` 下的文件
- **严禁**修改 `apps/miniapp/`、`apps/web-vue/`、`apps/admin-react/`、`backend/`、`proto/`、`gen/`、`docs/`、
  `scripts/`、`deploy/`、`design-tokens/`。其他 worker 正在并行修改这些目录，你改会冲突。
- 设计令牌在 `design-tokens/generated/`（只读）。新增页面必须用已有令牌常量，不要硬编码颜色和间距。

## 现有代码结构速览（先摸清再动手）

```
apps/flutter/lib/
├── api/          # api_client.dart auth_api.dart cart_api.dart invoice_api.dart
│                 # json_key_converter.dart logistics_api.dart marketing_api.dart
│                 # notification_api.dart order_api.dart payment_api.dart points_api.dart
│                 # product_api.dart token_store.dart
├── core/         # 主题、路由、常量
├── mock/         # Mock 数据
├── models/       # 数据模型
├── pages/        # 24 个 *_page.dart（你要扩到 80 个）
├── providers/    # Riverpod
├── utils/        # 格式化、金额等工具
└── widgets/      # 共享组件
```

**先读** `lib/pages/` 下 3-4 个已有页面（如 `home_page.dart`、`category_page.dart`、
`order_list_page.dart`、`product_detail_page.dart`）摸清既有风格：
**Flutter 3 + Riverpod + go_router + dio**、Material 3、ConsumerWidget / ConsumerStatefulWidget、
复用 `lib/widgets/` 的共享组件与 `lib/utils/` 的格式化工具。**不要另起炉灶写一套风格。**

## 关键实现要求

### 1. 路由注册

每个新 `*_page.dart` 都必须在 `lib/core/` 里的路由表（go_router 配置）注册。
先找到现有路由表文件（`lib/core/` 下），照现有写法加路由。

路由路径建议与 `docs/page-expansion-plan.md` 的页面名对应（如 `/channel/recommend`、
`/order/pending-pay`、`/after-sale/list`）。参数化页面用**路径参数**或 **query 参数**区分。

### 2. API 层

新页面需要的接口，在 `lib/api/` 对应域文件里加方法，复用 `api_client.dart` 的 dio 实例。

**特别注意 snake_case ↔ camelCase**：
项目已有 `lib/api/json_key_converter.dart` 拦截器，**请求方向自动 camel→snake、
响应方向自动 snake→camel**。你写的 Dart 代码里统一用 **camelCase** 字段名即可，
不需要手动转换，也不要绕过这个拦截器自己写转换逻辑。

分页参数用**点号嵌套**形式：`page.page=1&page.page_size=20`（现有代码里已有对应写法，照抄）。

### 3. Mock 兜底（必须）

后端部分接口可能还没就绪。沿用现有 Mock 机制（`lib/mock/`）。
新接口要有 Mock 数据，结构与真实接口一致（注意：Mock 数据在 Dart 侧也用 camelCase，
因为拦截器只在网络层做转换）。页面在 Mock 模式下要显示「演示数据模式」提示
（先看现有页面怎么做的，复用同一套逻辑/组件）。

### 4. 金额

后端返回**分**（int64）。展示必须用 `lib/utils/` 里已有的金额格式化工具
（形如 `formatYuan(cents)`），禁止直接展示 `12345` 这种数字。

### 5. 禁止事项

- 禁止 TODO / FIXME / 「敬请期待」/「开发中」等占位文案
- 禁止空 `onPressed` / `onTap` 死按钮
- 禁止复制粘贴同一页面改标题充数 —— 参数化页面必须**共用组件**，通过构造参数/路由参数区分
- 禁止无意义空状态（空状态必须有引导操作）

### 6. 端口提示（重要，别误判）

本机 **8080 端口被无关进程占用**（进程名 `books-api`，非本项目）。
本地连后端用 **8090**。先看 `lib/api/api_client.dart` 里 baseUrl 怎么配的
（可能有 `String.fromEnvironment` 或常量），按现有方式覆盖为 `http://127.0.0.1:8090`。
后端健康检查：`curl -s http://127.0.0.1:8090/healthz` 返回 `{"status":"ok"}` 即正常。
如果看到「登录被拦截 / 401」，先确认后端是否在跑，**不要去改后端代码**。

## 验收（必须自检并在最终回复中给出实际输出）

```bash
cd /<WORKSPACE>/shopstack/apps/flutter
flutter analyze
flutter test
```

另外自查并报告：
- 页面数：`ls lib/pages/*/*_page.dart | wc -l`（注意现有页面是 `lib/pages/<name>_page.dart` 还是
  `lib/pages/<name>/<name>_page.dart`，按实际目录结构统计）—— 目标 80
- 路由数与页面数是否匹配（每个页面都有路由）
- `flutter analyze` 的完整输出（必须 0 error / 0 warning）
- `flutter test` 的完整输出（必须全绿）

## 交付要求

最终回复里给出：
1. 新增的 56 个页面逐个列出（dart 文件名 + 中文页面名）
2. 新建/修改了哪些文件（路径列表）
3. 上面几条自检命令的**实际输出**（粘贴真实结果，不要写"应该没问题"）
4. 页面数 / 路由数统计结果
5. 诚实说明没做完或有疑问的部分

**禁止虚报**。没做完就说没做完。

<details><summary>🛠 read</summary>

````
1→# 阶段 3 前端 worker 通用须知
     2→
     3→以下 4 个 brief 共同引用本文件内容。派发时请把本文件路径告诉 worker：
     4→`docs/page-expansion-plan.md`（页面清单，唯一来源）
     5→
     6→## 共同背景
     7→
     8→ShopStack 是中大型电商项目：Go 微服务后端（9 服务）+ Flutter 移动端 + 微信小程序 +
     9→Vue3 Web 用户端 + React 后台管理端。本轮任务是把**每一端**从当前页面数扩到 **80 页**。
    10→
    11→工作目录：`/<WORKSPACE>/shopstack`
    12→
    13→## 页面清单来源
    14→
    15→**必须先读 `docs/page-expansion-plan.md`**，里面有每一端需要新增的页面的完整清单
    16→（编号、页面名、文件路径、数据来源接口）。严格按清单实现，不要自行增删页面。
    17→
    18→## 目录所有权（互不重叠，禁止越界）
    19→
    20→| Worker | 可写目录 | 禁止触碰 |
    21→|---|---|---|
    22→| Flutter | `apps/flutter/**` | 其他三个前端目录、backend/、proto/、gen/、docs/、scripts/、deploy/ |
    23→| 小程序 | `apps/miniapp/**` | 同上 |
    24→| Vue3 | `apps/web-vue/**` | 同上 |
    25→| React | `apps/admin-react/**` | 同上 |
    26→
    27→各 worker 之间**不得互相修改对方的目录**。后端目录 `backend/` 已被阶段 2 的 worker 修改，
    28→前端 worker 一律不得改动。
    29→
    30→## 硬性规则（违反即返工）
    31→
    32→### 1. 禁止占位
    33→
    34→- 禁止 TODO / FIXME / 「敬请期待」/「开发中」/「功能上线中」等占位文案
    35→- 禁止空 `onClick` / `@click` 不带处理函数 / 点了没反应的死按钮
    36→- 禁止「暂无数据」之外的无意义空状态（空状态必须有引导操作）
    37→- 禁止复制粘贴同一个页面改标题充数（参数化页面必须共用组件，只有路径/标题/筛选参数不同）
    38→
    39→### 2. Mock 兜底（必须）
    40→
    41→后端可能还没就绪或部分接口未实现。前端必须有 Mock 兜底：
    42→- 请求失败 / `code != 0` / 超时 → 回落到本地 Mock 数据
    43→- 页面顶部显示「演示数据模式」提示（各端已有 `DemoBanner` / `DemoModeAlert` 等组件，沿用）
    44→- Mock 数据的 JSON 结构必须与真实接口**完全一致**（snake_case 字段名）
    45→
    46→各端 Mock 机制位置：
    47→- Flutter：`lib/mock/`，API 层 `lib/api/*.dart`
    48→- 小程序：`utils/mock.js`（内存态，写操作会真实改变状态），`utils/request.js` 里已有兜底逻辑
    49→- Vue3：`src/api/client.ts` 里有 Mock 分支，各 `src/api/*.ts` 提供数据
    50→- React：`src/mock/`（`data.ts` + `index.ts`），`src/store/mock` 控制开关
    51→
    52→### 3. 金额
    53→
    54→后端返回**分**（int64）。展示时必须用各端已有的 `formatYuan(cents)`（Vue3/小程序）
    55→或 `PriceText` 组件（Vue3）/ 等价工具（Flutter `lib/utils/`、React `src/utils/`）。
    56→禁止直接展示 `12345` 这种数字。
    57→
    58→### 4. 接口调用规范
    59→
    60→- 分页 query 用**点号嵌套**：`?page.page=1&page.page_size=20`
    61→- 响应信封：`{ code, message, data, trace_id }`，`code === 0` 为成功
    62→- JSON 字段名 **snake_case**（与 proto 一致）
    63→- 64 位整数是 JSON **数字**
    64→
    65→### 5. 端口提示（重要，别误判）
    66→
    67→本机 **8080 端口被无关进程占用**（进程名 `books-api`，不是本项目）。
    68→本地连后端请用 **8090**：
    69→```bash
    70→VITE_GATEWAY_URL=http://127.0.0.1:8090     # Vue3
    71→VITE_API_BASE_URL=http://127.0.0.1:8090    # React
    72→```
    73→如果你连 8090 看到「登录被拦截 / 401」，先确认后端是否在跑：
    74→```bash
    75→curl -s http://127.0.0.1:8090/healthz
    76→```
    77→返回 `{"status":"ok"}` 说明后端正常。**不要因此去改后端代码**。
    78→
    79→### 6. 设计令牌
    80→
    81→设计令牌在 `design-tokens/generated/`，各端已有对应的样式文件（CSS 变量 / TS 常量 /
    82→Dart 常量 / WXSS 变量）。新增页面必须用已有令牌，**不要硬编码颜色和间距**。
    83→
    84→### 7. 视觉与响应式
    85→
    86→- 移动端（Flutter / 小程序）：375px 宽下无横向滚动、无元素重叠
    87→- Web（Vue3 / React）：1440px 宽下布局正常；后台表格在窄屏下可横向滚动
    88→- 沿用各端现有的卡片、列表、表格、空状态、加载态组件
    89→
    90→### 8. 交付自检（必须在最终回复里给出实际输出）
    91→
    92→每个 worker 交付时必须报告：
    93→1. 新增了哪些页面（逐个列出）
    94→2. 新建/修改了哪些文件（路径列表）
    95→3. 自检命令的**实际输出**（不是"应该没问题"）
    96→4. 页面数统计结果（用什么命令数的）
    97→5. 诚实说明没做完或有疑问的部分
    98→
    99→**禁止虚报**。如果某个页面没做完或某个命令失败了，如实说。
   100→
   101→## 验收命令速查
   102→
   103→```bash
   104→# Flutter
   105→cd apps/flutter && flutter analyze          # 必须 0 error 0 warning
   106→cd apps/flutter && flutter test             # 必须全绿

   111→# 校验 app.json pages[] + subPackages[].pages[] 合计 == 80
   112→# 每页 .js/.json/.wxml/.wxss 四件套齐全且 JSON 可解析
   113→# 所有 bindtap/catchtap 绑定的事件都有对应处理函数
   114→
   115→# Vue3
   116→cd apps/web-vue && npm run build            # 必须成功
   117→# views 数 == routes 数 == 80
   118→
   119→# React
   120→cd apps/admin-react && npm run build        # 必须成功
   121→# pages 数（含 system/）== routes 数 == 80
   122→```

final routerProvider = Provider<GoRouter>((ref) {
  final gate = ref.watch(authGateProvider);
  return GoRouter(
    initialLocation: AppRoutes.splash,
    refreshListenable: gate,
    redirect: (context, state) {
      final auth = ref.read(authProvider);
      final location = state.matchedLocation;
      if (location == AppRoutes.splash) return null;
      if (auth.bootstrapping) return AppRoutes.splash;
      if (!auth.authenticated) {
        return location == AppRoutes.login ? null : AppRoutes.login;
      }
      if (location == AppRoutes.login) return AppRoutes.home;
      return null;
    },
    routes: [
      GoRoute(
        path: AppRoutes.splash,
        builder: (context, state) => const SplashPage(),
      ),
      GoRoute(
        path: AppRoutes.login,
        builder: (context, state) => const LoginPage(),
      ),
      StatefulShellRoute.indexedStack(
        builder: (context, state, navigationShell) => AppShell(
          navigationShell: navigationShell,
        ),
        branches: [
          StatefulShellBranch(
            routes: [
              GoRoute(
                path: AppRoutes.home,
                builder: (context, state) => const HomePage(),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          StatefulShellBranch(
            routes: [

1→import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
     2→
     3→import '../core/constants/api_constants.dart';
     4→import '../models/user.dart';
     5→import 'json_key_converter.dart';
     6→import 'token_store.dart';
     7→
     8→/// 业务错误：后端返回 `code !== 0`。
     9→class ApiException implements Exception {
    10→  ApiException(this.code, this.message);
    11→
    12→  final int code;
    13→  final String message;
    14→
    15→  @override
    16→  String toString() => message;
    17→}
    18→
    19→/// 网络错误：后端不可达、超时等。
    20→class NetworkException implements Exception {
    21→  NetworkException(this.message);
    22→
    23→  final String message;
    24→
    25→  @override
    26→  String toString() => message;
    27→}
    28→
    29→/// Dio 客户端：统一注入鉴权头、解包响应信封、处理 401 静默刷新。
    30→class ApiClient {
    31→  ApiClient({required this.tokens, Dio? dio, String? baseUrl})
    32→      : dio = dio ?? Dio() {
    33→    this.dio.options = BaseOptions(
    34→      baseUrl: baseUrl ?? ApiConstants.baseUrlWithPrefix,
    35→      connectTimeout: ApiConstants.connectTimeout,
    36→      receiveTimeout: ApiConstants.receiveTimeout,
    37→      sendTimeout: ApiConstants.sendTimeout,
    38→      headers: const {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
    39→    );
    40→    this.dio.interceptors.add(
    41→      InterceptorsWrapper(
    42→        onRequest: (options, handler) {
    43→          final token = tokens.accessToken;
    44→          if (token != null && token.isNotEmpty) {
    45→            options.headers['Authorization'] = 'Bearer $token';
    46→          }
    47→          // 请求体统一转成 snake_case（已是 snake_case 的键保持不变）。
    48→          if (options.data != null) {
    49→            options.data = JsonKeyConverter.camelToSnake(options.data);
    50→          }
    51→          handler.next(options);
    52→        },
    53→        onResponse: (response, handler) {
    54→          // 响应体统一转成 camelCase，App 内的 Model 保持 camelCase 字段。
    55→          final data = response.data;
    56→          if (data is Map || data is List) {
    57→            response.data = JsonKeyConverter.snakeToCamel(data);
    58→          }
    59→          handler.next(response);
    60→        },
    61→        onError: _handleError,
    62→      ),
    63→    );
    64→  }
    65→
    66→  final Dio dio;
    67→  final TokenStore tokens;
    68→
    69→  Future<void> _handleError(
    70→      DioException error, ErrorInterceptorHandler handler) async {
    71→    final response = error.response;
    72→    final request = error.requestOptions;
    73→    if (response?.statusCode == 401 && request.extra['retried'] != true) {
    74→      final newToken = await refreshAccessToken();
    75→      if (newToken != null && newToken.isNotEmpty) {
    76→        request.extra['retried'] = true;
    77→        request.headers['Authorization'] = 'Bearer $newToken';
    78→        try {
    79→          final retried = await dio.fetch<dynamic>(request);
    80→          return handler.resolve(retried);
    81→        } catch (e) {
    82→          return handler.next(error);
    83→        }
    84→      }
    85→    }
    86→    return handler.next(error);
    87→  }
    88→
    89→  Future<String?>? _refreshing;
    90→
    91→  /// 使用 refresh_token 静默刷新访问令牌。
    92→  Future<String?> refreshAccessToken() {
    93→    final pending = _refreshing;
    94→    if (pending != null) return pending;
    95→    final future = _doRefresh().whenComplete(() {
    96→      _refreshing = null;
    97→    });
    98→    _refreshing = future;
    99→    return future;
   100→  }
   101→
   102→  Future<String?> _doRefresh() async {
   103→    final refreshToken = tokens.refreshToken;
   104→    if (refreshToken == null || refreshToken.isEmpty) return null;
   105→    try {
   106→      final plain = Dio(BaseOptions(
   107→        baseUrl: ApiConstants.baseUrlWithPrefix,
   108→        connectTimeout: ApiConstants.connectTimeout,
   109→        receiveTimeout: ApiConstants.receiveTimeout,
   110→        headers: const {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
   111→      ));
   112→      final res = await plain.post<dynamic>(
   113→        '/auth/refresh',
   114→        data: {'refresh_token': refreshToken},
   115→      );
   116→      final data = _unwrap(res.data);
   117→      final session = AuthSession.fromJson(data);
   118→      if (session.accessToken.isEmpty) return null;
   119→      await tokens.saveSession(
   120→        accessToken: session.accessToken,
   121→        refreshToken: session.refreshToken.isEmpty
   122→            ? refreshToken
   123→            : session.refreshToken,
   124→        user: session.user,
   125→      );
   126→      return session.accessToken;
   127→    } catch (_) {
   128→      return null;
   129→    }
   130→  }
   131→
   132→  /// 解包统一响应信封，失败时抛出异常。
   133→  Map<String, dynamic> _unwrap(dynamic body) {
   134→    if (body is Map) {
   135→      final map = body.map((key, value) => MapEntry(key.toString(), value));
   136→      if (map.containsKey('code')) {
   137→        final code = map['code'];
   138→        final codeInt = code is int ? code : int.tryParse('$code') ?? -1;
   139→        if (codeInt != 0) {
   140→          final message = map['message']?.toString() ?? '请求失败，请稍后重试';
   141→          throw ApiException(codeInt, message);
   142→        }
   143→        final data = map['data'];
   144→        if (data is Map) {
   145→          return data.map((key, value) => MapEntry(key.toString(), value));
   146→        }
   147→        if (data is List) {
   148→          return {'items': data};
   149→        }
   150→        return <String, dynamic>{};
   151→      }
   152→      return map;
   153→    }
   154→    return <String, dynamic>{};
   155→  }
   156→
   157→  Future<Map<String, dynamic>> get(String path,
   158→      {Map<String, dynamic>? query}) async {
   159→    try {
   160→      final res = await dio.get<dynamic>(path, queryParameters: query);
   161→      return _unwrap(res.data);
   162→    } on DioException catch (e) {
   163→      throw _toNetworkError(e);
   164→    }
   165→  }
   166→
   167→  Future<Map<String, dynamic>> post(String path, {Object? body}) async {
   168→    try {
   169→      final res = await dio.post<dynamic>(path, data: body);
   170→      return _unwrap(res.data);
   171→    } on DioException catch (e) {
   172→      throw _toNetworkError(e);
   173→    }
   174→  }
   175→
   176→  Future<Map<String, dynamic>> put(String path, {Object? body}) async {
   177→    try {
   178→      final res = await dio.put<dynamic>(path, data: body);
   179→      return _unwrap(res.data);
   180→    } on DioException catch (e) {
   181→      throw _toNetworkError(e);
   182→    }
   183→  }
   184→
   185→  Future<Map<String, dynamic>> delete(String path,
   186→      {Map<String, dynamic>? query}) async {
   187→    try {
   188→      final res = await dio.delete<dynamic>(path, queryParameters: query);
   189→      return _unwrap(res.data);
   190→    } on DioException catch (e) {
   191→      throw _toNetworkError(e);
   192→    }
   193→  }
   194→
   195→  /// 提取服务端返回的业务错误信息。
   196→  String _serverMessage(DioException error) {
   197→    final data = error.response?.data;
   198→    if (data is Map) {
   199→      final message = data['message'];
   200→      if (message != null && message.toString().isNotEmpty) {
   201→        return message.toString();
   202→      }
   203→    }
   204→    return error.message ?? '请求失败';
   205→  }
   206→
   207→  Exception _toNetworkError(DioException error) {
   208→    final type = error.type;
   209→    if (type == DioExceptionType.connectionTimeout ||
   210→        type == DioExceptionType.receiveTimeout ||
   211→        type == DioExceptionType.sendTimeout ||
   212→        type == DioExceptionType.connectionError) {
   213→      return NetworkException('网络连接失败，请检查后端服务是否已启动');
   214→    }
   215→    if (error.response?.statusCode == 401) {
   216→      return ApiException(401, '登录已过期，请重新登录');
   217→    }
   218→    // 业务错误统一走信封：4xx 响应体里同样带 code / message。
   219→    final body = error.response?.data;
   220→    if (body is Map && body.containsKey('code')) {
   221→      final code = body['code'];
   222→      final codeInt = code is int ? code : int.tryParse('$code') ?? -1;
   223→      if (codeInt != 0) {
   224→        return ApiException(codeInt, body['message']?.toString() ?? '请求失败');
   225→      }
   226→    }
   227→    if (error.response?.statusCode == 403) {
   228→      return ApiException(403, '没有访问权限');
   229→    }
   230→    final status = error.response?.statusCode;
   231→    if (status != null && status >= 500) {
   232→      return NetworkException('服务异常（$status），请稍后重试');
   233→    }
   234→    if (status != null && status >= 400) {
   235→      return NetworkException('请求被拒绝（$status）：${_serverMessage(error)}');
   236→    }
   237→    return NetworkException(_serverMessage(error));
   238→  }
   239→}

=== lib/core/constants/api_constants.dart ===
/// 网关地址与请求常量。
class ApiConstants {
  ApiConstants._();

  /// 后端网关地址，可通过 `--dart-define=API_BASE_URL=...` 覆盖。
  static const String baseUrl = String.fromEnvironment(
    'API_BASE_URL',
    defaultValue: 'http://localhost:8080',
  );

  /// 接口前缀。
  static const String prefix = '/api/v1';

  /// 完整的基础地址。
  static const String baseUrlWithPrefix = '$baseUrl$prefix';

  static const Duration connectTimeout = Duration(seconds: 8);
  static const Duration receiveTimeout = Duration(seconds: 15);
  static const Duration sendTimeout = Duration(seconds: 15);

  /// 默认分页大小。
  static const int defaultPageSize = 20;

  /// 本地存储键。
  static const String keyAccessToken = 'shopstack_access_token';
  static const String keyRefreshToken = 'shopstack_refresh_token';
  static const String keyUser = 'shopstack_user';
}

import 'package:intl/intl.dart';

/// 金额单位为「分」，统一格式化为「元」。
class Money {
  Money._();

  /// 分 -> 元字符串，保留两位小数。
  static String formatYuan(int cents) {
    final negative = cents < 0;
    final value = (cents.abs() / 100).toStringAsFixed(2);
    return negative ? '-$value' : value;
  }

  /// 分 -> 带货币符号的金额，如 `¥4299.00`。
  static String formatYuanWithSymbol(int cents) => '¥${formatYuan(cents)}';

  /// 分 -> 去掉小数尾零的金额，如 `¥4299`。
  static String formatYuanCompact(int cents) {
    final yuan = cents / 100;
    if (yuan == yuan.roundToDouble()) {
      return '¥${yuan.round()}';
    }
    return '¥${yuan.toStringAsFixed(2)}';
  }
}

/// 金额格式化扩展。
extension MoneyFormat on int {
  String get formatYuan => Money.formatYuan(this);
  String get formatYuanWithSymbol => Money.formatYuanWithSymbol(this);
}

/// 时间格式化工具，输入为 RFC3339 字符串。
class DateFormatter {
  DateFormatter._();

  static final DateFormat _date = DateFormat('yyyy-MM-dd');
  static final DateFormat _dateTime = DateFormat('yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm');
  static final DateFormat _dateTimeSec = DateFormat('yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss');
  static final DateFormat _monthDay = DateFormat('MM-dd');
  static final DateFormat _clock = DateFormat('HH:mm');

  static DateTime? parse(String? raw) {
    if (raw == null || raw.isEmpty) return null;
    return DateTime.tryParse(raw);
  }

  /// `2026-10-20 14:30`
  static String dateTime(String? raw, {String fallback = '--'}) {
    final time = parse(raw);
    if (time == null) return fallback;
    return _dateTime.format(time.toLocal());
  }

  /// `2026-10-20 14:30:12`
  static String dateTimeSec(String? raw, {String fallback = '--'}) {
    final time = parse(raw);
    if (time == null) return fallback;
    return _dateTimeSec.format(time.toLocal());
  }

  /// `2026-10-20`
  static String date(String? raw, {String fallback = '--'}) {
    final time = parse(raw);
    if (time == null) return fallback;
    return _date.format(time.toLocal());
  }

  /// `10-20`
  static String monthDay(String? raw, {String fallback = '--'}) {
    final time = parse(raw);
    if (time == null) return fallback;
    return _monthDay.format(time.toLocal());
  }

  /// `14:30`
  static String clock(String? raw, {String fallback = '--'}) {
    final time = parse(raw);
    if (time == null) return fallback;
    return _clock.format(time.toLocal());
  }

  /// 相对时间：刚刚 / 5 分钟前 / 3 天前
  static String relative(String? raw, {String fallback = '--'}) {
    final time = parse(raw);
    if (time == null) return fallback;
    final diff = DateTime.now().difference(time.toLocal());
    if (diff.inSeconds < 60) return '刚刚';
    if (diff.inMinutes < 60) return '${diff.inMinutes} 分钟前';
    if (diff.inHours < 24) return '${diff.inHours} 小时前';
    if (diff.inDays < 30) return '${diff.inDays} 天前';
    return date(raw, fallback: fallback);
  }

  /// Unix 秒时间戳 -> `yyyy-MM-dd`
  static String fromUnix(int? seconds, {String fallback = '--'}) {
    if (seconds == null || seconds <= 0) return fallback;
    return _date.format(DateTime.fromMillisecondsSinceEpoch(seconds * 1000).toLocal());
  }
}

/// 销量文本：超过 1 万显示「x.x万」。
class SalesFormatter {
  SalesFormatter._();

  static String format(int sales) {
    if (sales >= 10000) {
      return '${(sales / 10000).toStringAsFixed(1)}万';
    }
    return '$sales';
  }
}

/// 与 shopstack.common.v1 中 proto 枚举一一对应的 Dart 枚举。

/// JSON 中枚举既可能是字符串（`"ORDER_STATUS_PAID"`），也可能是 proto 序号（2），
/// 统一通过 [parseEnumValue] 解析。
library;

enum Gender {
  unspecified(0, 'GENDER_UNSPECIFIED', '未知'),
  male(1, 'GENDER_MALE', '男'),
  female(2, 'GENDER_FEMALE', '女'),

  final String wire;
  final String label;
}

  offShelf(3, 'PRODUCT_STATUS_OFF_SHELF', '已下架');

  const ProductStatus(this.code, this.wire, this.label);
  final int code;
  final String wire;
  final String label;
}

enum OrderStatus {
  unspecified(0, 'ORDER_STATUS_UNSPECIFIED', '未知'),
  pendingPayment(1, 'ORDER_STATUS_PENDING_PAYMENT', '待付款'),
  paid(2, 'ORDER_STATUS_PAID', '待发货'),
  shipped(3, 'ORDER_STATUS_SHIPPED', '待收货'),
  completed(4, 'ORDER_STATUS_COMPLETED', '已完成'),
  cancelled(5, 'ORDER_STATUS_CANCELLED', '已取消'),
  refunding(6, 'ORDER_STATUS_REFUNDING', '退款中'),
  refunded(7, 'ORDER_STATUS_REFUNDED', '已退款'),
  closed(8, 'ORDER_STATUS_CLOSED', '已关闭');

  const OrderStatus(this.code, this.wire, this.label);

  final String label;

/// 将 Dart 枚举名转为 `pending_payment` 形式，便于与后端短名匹配。
String enumKey(String dartName) => dartName
    .replaceAllMapped(RegExp(r'([A-Z])'), (match) => '_${match[1]}')
    .toUpperCase()
    .replaceFirst('_', '');

/// 解析后端枚举值：支持字符串（`ORDER_STATUS_PAID` / `PAID`）与 proto 序号。
///
/// [raw] 为后端返回的原始值，[values] 为该枚举的全部取值。
T parseEnumValue<T>(
  dynamic raw,
  List<T> values,
  String Function(T) wireOf,
  String Function(T) keyOf,
) {
  if (raw == null) return values.first;
  if (raw is int) {
    if (raw >= 0 && raw < values.length) return values[raw];
    return values.first;
  }
  if (raw is num) {
    final index = raw.toInt();
    if (index >= 0 && index < values.length) return values[index];
    return values.first;
  }
  final text = raw.toString().trim();
  if (text.isEmpty) return values.first;
  for (final value in values) {
    if (wireOf(value).toUpperCase() == text.toUpperCase()) return value;
  }
  final normalized = text.replaceAll(RegExp(r'[^A-Za-z0-9]'), '').toLowerCase();
  for (final value in values) {
    final key = keyOf(value).toLowerCase();
    if (key == normalized || key.endsWith(normalized)) return value;
  }
  return values.first;
}

/// 订单状态到中文的扩展。
extension OrderStatusLabel on OrderStatus {
  String get text => label;
  bool get isPendingPayment => this == OrderStatus.pendingPayment;
  bool get isPaid => this == OrderStatus.paid;
  bool get isShipped => this == OrderStatus.shipped;
  bool get isFinished =>
      this == OrderStatus.completed || this == OrderStatus.cancelled ||
      this == OrderStatus.refunded || this == OrderStatus.closed;
  bool get isRefunding => this == OrderStatus.refunding;
}

/// 商品状态扩展。
extension ProductStatusLabel on ProductStatus {
  String get text => label;
}

/// 支付状态扩展。
extension PaymentStatusLabel on PaymentStatus {
  String get text => label;
  bool get isSuccess => this == PaymentStatus.success;
}

/// 支付渠道扩展。
extension PaymentChannelLabel on PaymentChannel {
  String get text => label;
}

/// 优惠券类型扩展。
extension CouponTypeLabel on CouponType {
  String get text => label;
}

/// 用户券状态扩展。
extension UserCouponStatusLabel on UserCouponStatus {
  String get text => label;
}

/// 物流状态扩展。
extension ShipmentStatusLabel on ShipmentStatus {
  String get text => label;
}

/// 通知类型扩展。
extension NotificationTypeLabel on NotificationType {
  String get text => label;
}

/// 用户状态扩展。
extension UserStatusLabel on UserStatus {
  String get text => label;
}

/// 性别扩展。
extension GenderLabel on Gender {
  String get text => label;
}

/// 评价状态扩展。
extension ReviewStatusLabel on ReviewStatus {
  String get text => label;
}
=== lib/utils/json_util.dart ===
/// JSON 解析辅助工具。
///
/// 后端网关可能以 snake_case 或 camelCase 输出字段（protojson 默认 camelCase，
/// 网关层多转换为 snake_case），因此所有模型统一走这里的取值逻辑。
class JsonUtil {
  JsonUtil._();

  static Map<String, dynamic> asMap(dynamic value) {
    if (value is Map) {
      return value.map((key, val) => MapEntry(key.toString(), val));
    }
    return <String, dynamic>{};
  }

  static List<Map<String, dynamic>> asMapList(dynamic value) {
    if (value is List) {
      return value.whereType<Map>().map(asMap).toList();
    }
    return <Map<String, dynamic>>[];
  }

  static List<String> asStringList(dynamic value) {
    if (value is List) {
      return value.map((e) => e?.toString() ?? '').toList();
    }
    return <String>[];
  }

  static List<int> asIntList(dynamic value) {
    if (value is List) {
      return value.map((e) {
        if (e is int) return e;
        if (e is num) return e.toInt();
        return int.tryParse(e?.toString() ?? '') ?? 0;
      }).toList();
    }
    return <int>[];
  }

  static Map<String, String> asStringMap(dynamic value) {
    if (value is Map) {
      return value.map((key, val) => MapEntry(key.toString(), val?.toString() ?? ''));
    }
    return <String, String>{};
  }

  static int intOf(Map<String, dynamic> json, String key, [int fallback = 0]) {
    final value = json[key];
    if (value is int) return value;
    if (value is num) return value.toInt();
    if (value is String) return int.tryParse(value) ?? fallback;
    return fallback;
  }

  static double doubleOf(Map<String, dynamic> json, String key, [double fallback = 0]) {
    final value = json[key];
    if (value is double) return value;
    if (value is num) return value.toDouble();
    if (value is String) return double.tryParse(value) ?? fallback;
    return fallback;
  }

  static String stringOf(Map<String, dynamic> json, String key, [String fallback = '']) {
    final value = json[key];
    if (value == null) return fallback;
    if (value is String) return value;
    return value.toString();
  }

  static bool boolOf(Map<String, dynamic> json, String key, [bool fallback = false]) {
    final value = json[key];
    if (value is bool) return value;
    if (value is num) return value != 0;
    if (value is String) return value == 'true' || value == '1';
    return fallback;
  }

  /// 优先取 snake_case 字段，缺失时回退 camelCase。
  static dynamic valueOf(Map<String, dynamic> json, String snake, String camel) {
    final snakeValue = json[snake];
    if (snakeValue != null) return snakeValue;
    return json[camel];
  }
}

=== lib/widgets/app_card.dart ===
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../core/theme/app_theme.dart';

/// 通用卡片：白底 + 圆角 + 柔和阴影。
class AppCard extends StatelessWidget {
  const AppCard({
    super.key,
    required this.child,
    this.padding = const EdgeInsets.all(12),
    this.margin,
    this.radius,
    this.onTap,
    this.color,
    this.shadow = true,
  });

  final Widget child;
  final EdgeInsetsGeometry padding;
  final EdgeInsetsGeometry? margin;
  final double? radius;
  final VoidCallback? onTap;
  final Color? color;
  final bool shadow;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final content = Container(
      padding: padding,
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: color ?? AppTheme.bgCard,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(radius ?? AppTheme.radiusLg),
        boxShadow: shadow ? AppTheme.shadowSm : null,
      ),
      child: child,
    );
    if (margin != null) {
      return Padding(padding: margin!, child: content);
    }
    if (onTap == null) return content;
    return Material(
      color: Colors.transparent,
      child: InkWell(
        onTap: onTap,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(radius ?? AppTheme.radiusLg),
        child: content,
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// 区块标题：左侧标题 + 右侧更多。
class SectionHeader extends StatelessWidget {
  const SectionHeader({
    super.key,
    required this.title,
    this.subtitle,
    this.actionText,
    this.onAction,
    this.padding = const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(12, 16, 12, 8),
  });

  final String title;
  final String? subtitle;
  final String? actionText;
  final VoidCallback? onAction;
  final EdgeInsetsGeometry padding;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Padding(
      padding: padding,
      child: Row(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.center,
        children: [
          Container(
            width: 4,
            height: 16,
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: AppTheme.brand,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(2),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 8),
          Text(title, style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleMedium),
          if (subtitle != null) ...[
            const SizedBox(width: 8),
            Text(subtitle!, style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.labelSmall),
          ],
          const Spacer(),
          if (actionText != null)
            GestureDetector(
              onTap: onAction,
              child: Row(
                children: [
                  Text(
                    actionText!,
                    style: TextStyle(
                        fontSize: 13, color: AppTheme.textTertiary),
                  ),
                  Icon(Icons.chevron_right_rounded,
                      size: 16, color: AppTheme.textTertiary),
                ],
              ),
            ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// 状态标签。
class StatusTag extends StatelessWidget {
  const StatusTag({
    super.key,
    required this.text,
    this.color,
    this.background,
    this.icon,
  });

  final String text;
  final Color? color;
  final Color? background;
  final IconData? icon;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final foreground = color ?? AppTheme.brand;
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 3),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: background ?? foreground.withValues(alpha: 0.10),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppTheme.radiusSm),
      ),
      child: Row(
        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
        children: [
          if (icon != null) ...[
            Icon(icon, size: 12, color: foreground),
            const SizedBox(width: 3),
          ],
          Text(
            text,
            style: TextStyle(
              fontSize: 12,
              fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
              color: foreground,
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}
=== lib/widgets/app_image.dart ===
import 'package:cached_network_image/cached_network_image.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../core/theme/app_theme.dart';

/// 统一的网络图片组件：自带占位、错误占位与圆角。
class AppImage extends StatelessWidget {
  const AppImage({
    super.key,
    required this.url,
    this.width,
    this.height,
    this.fit = BoxFit.cover,
    this.radius = 0,
    this.icon = Icons.image_not_supported_outlined,
  });

  final String url;
  final double? width;
  final double? height;
  final BoxFit fit;
  final double radius;
  final IconData icon;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final placeholder = Container(
      width: width,
      height: height,
      color: AppTheme.bgMuted,
      alignment: Alignment.center,
      child: Icon(icon, size: 20, color: AppTheme.textTertiary),
    );
    Widget image;
    if (url.isEmpty) {
      image = placeholder;
    } else {
      image = CachedNetworkImage(
        imageUrl: url,
        width: width,
        height: height,
        fit: fit,
        placeholder: (context, url) => Container(
          width: width,
          height: height,
          color: AppTheme.bgMuted,
        ),
        errorWidget: (context, url, error) => placeholder,
      );
    }
    if (radius <= 0) return image;
    return ClipRRect(
      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(radius),
      child: image,
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';

import '../core/theme/app_theme.dart';
import '../providers/cart_provider.dart';

/// 底部 Tab 导航外壳。
class AppShell extends ConsumerWidget {
  const AppShell({super.key, required this.navigationShell});

  final StatefulNavigationShell navigationShell;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) {
    final cartCount = ref.watch(cartCountProvider);
    return Scaffold(
      body: navigationShell,
      bottomNavigationBar: Container(
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          border: Border(top: BorderSide(color: AppTheme.borderSubtle, width: 0.5)),
        ),
        child: NavigationBar(
          selectedIndex: navigationShell.currentIndex,
          onDestinationSelected: (index) {
            navigationShell.goBranch(
              index,
              initialLocation: index == navigationShell.currentIndex,
            );
          },
          destinations: [
            const NavigationDestination(
              icon: Icon(Icons.home_outlined),
              selectedIcon: Icon(Icons.home_rounded),
              label: '首页',
            ),
            const NavigationDestination(
              icon: Icon(Icons.grid_view_outlined),
              selectedIcon: Icon(Icons.grid_view_rounded),
              label: '分类',
            ),
            NavigationDestination(
              icon: _CartIcon(count: cartCount, selected: false),
              selectedIcon: _CartIcon(count: cartCount, selected: true),
              label: '购物车',
            ),
            const NavigationDestination(
              icon: Icon(Icons.person_outline_rounded),
              selectedIcon: Icon(Icons.person_rounded),
              label: '我的',
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// 带角标的购物车图标。
class _CartIcon extends StatelessWidget {
  const _CartIcon({required this.count, required this.selected});

  final int count;
  final bool selected;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final icon = Icon(
      selected ? Icons.shopping_cart_rounded : Icons.shopping_cart_outlined,
    );
    if (count <= 0) return icon;
    return Stack(
      clipBehavior: Clip.none,
      children: [
        icon,
        Positioned(
          right: -6,
          top: -4,
          child: Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 4, vertical: 1),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: AppTheme.danger,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
            ),
            constraints: const BoxConstraints(minWidth: 15),
            child: Text(
              count > 99 ? '99+' : '$count',
              textAlign: TextAlign.center,
              style: const TextStyle(
                fontSize: 10,
                height: 1.2,
                color: Colors.white,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

/// 演示数据模式提示条：后端不可用时展示在页面顶部。
class DemoBanner extends ConsumerWidget {
  const DemoBanner({super.key, this.message});

  final String? message;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) {
    final demo = ref.watch(demoModeProvider);
    if (!demo) return const SizedBox.shrink();
    return Container(
      width: double.infinity,
      color: AppTheme.warning.withValues(alpha: 0.14),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 8),
      child: SafeArea(
        bottom: false,
        child: Row(
          children: [
            Icon(Icons.cloud_off_rounded, size: 16, color: AppTheme.warning700),
            const SizedBox(width: 6),
            Expanded(
              child: Text(
                message ?? '演示数据模式：后端服务未连接，当前展示内置演示数据',
                style: TextStyle(
                  fontSize: 12,
                  color: AppTheme.warning700,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../core/theme/app_theme.dart';
import '../utils/formatters.dart';

/// 价格文本：金额单位为「分」。
class PriceText extends StatelessWidget {
  const PriceText({
    super.key,
    required this.cents,
    this.size = 18,
    this.color,
    this.bold = true,
    this.showSymbol = true,
  });

  final int cents;
  final double size;
  final Color? color;
  final bool bold;
  final bool showSymbol;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final text = showSymbol
        ? Money.formatYuanWithSymbol(cents)
        : Money.formatYuan(cents);
    return Text(
      text,
      style: TextStyle(
        fontSize: size,
        height: 1.1,
        fontWeight: bold ? FontWeight.w700 : FontWeight.w500,
        color: color ?? AppTheme.danger,
        fontFeatures: const [FontFeature.tabularFigures()],
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// 原价（划线）。
class OriginalPriceText extends StatelessWidget {
  const OriginalPriceText({super.key, required this.cents, this.size = 12});

  final int cents;
  final double size;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Text(
      Money.formatYuanWithSymbol(cents),
      style: TextStyle(
        fontSize: size,
        color: AppTheme.textTertiary,
        decoration: TextDecoration.lineThrough,
        decorationColor: AppTheme.textTertiary,
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// 商品卡片：图片、标题、价格、销量、加购按钮。
class ProductCard extends StatelessWidget {
  const ProductCard({
    super.key,
    required this.product,
    this.onTap,
    this.onAddToCart,
    this.width,
  });

  final Product product;
  final VoidCallback? onTap;
  final VoidCallback? onAddToCart;
  final double? width;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      width: width,
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: AppTheme.bgCard,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppTheme.radiusLg),
        boxShadow: AppTheme.shadowSm,
      ),
      child: InkWell(
        onTap: onTap,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppTheme.radiusLg),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            ClipRRect(
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(
                  top: Radius.circular(AppTheme.radiusLg)),
              child: AspectRatio(
                aspectRatio: 1,
                child: AppImage(url: product.cover),
              ),
            ),
            Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(10, 10, 10, 10),
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Text(
                    product.title,
                    maxLines: 1,
                    overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                    style: TextStyle(
                      fontSize: 14,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                      color: AppTheme.textPrimary,
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 4),
                  Text(
                    product.subtitle,
                    maxLines: 1,
                    overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                    style: TextStyle(
                        fontSize: 12, color: AppTheme.textTertiary),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 8),
                  Row(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.baseline,
                    textBaseline: TextBaseline.alphabetic,
                    children: [
                      PriceText(cents: product.minPriceCents, size: 16),
                      if (product.hasDiscount) ...[
                        const SizedBox(width: 6),
                        OriginalPriceText(cents: product.maxPriceCents),
                      ],
                    ],
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 6),
                  Row(
                    children: [
                      Text(
                        '已售 ${SalesFormatter.format(product.salesCount)}',
                        style: TextStyle(
                            fontSize: 11, color: AppTheme.textTertiary),
                      ),
                      const Spacer(),
                      if (onAddToCart != null)
                        InkWell(
                          onTap: onAddToCart,
                          borderRadius:
                              BorderRadius.circular(AppTheme.radiusSm),
                          child: Container(
                            width: 26,
                            height: 26,
                            decoration: BoxDecoration(
                              color: AppTheme.brand,
                              borderRadius:
                                  BorderRadius.circular(AppTheme.radiusSm),
                            ),
                            child: const Icon(Icons.add_rounded,
                                size: 16, color: Colors.white),
                          ),
                        ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}
=== lib/widgets/quantity_stepper.dart ===
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../core/theme/app_theme.dart';

/// 数量步进器。
class QuantityStepper extends StatelessWidget {
  const QuantityStepper({
    super.key,
    required this.value,
    required this.onChanged,
    this.min = 1,
    this.max = 99,
    this.compact = false,
  });

  final int value;
  final ValueChanged<int> onChanged;
  final int min;
  final int max;
  final bool compact;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final size = compact ? 24.0 : 28.0;
    return Container(
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: AppTheme.bgMuted,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppTheme.radiusSm),
      ),
      child: Row(
        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
        children: [
          _StepButton(
            icon: Icons.remove,
            size: size,
            enabled: value > min,
            onTap: () => onChanged(value - 1),
          ),
          Container(
            width: compact ? 30 : 38,
            alignment: Alignment.center,
            child: Text(
              '$value',
              style: TextStyle(
                fontSize: 14,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                color: AppTheme.textPrimary,
              ),
            ),
          ),
          _StepButton(
            icon: Icons.add,
            size: size,
            enabled: value < max,
            onTap: () => onChanged(value + 1),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _StepButton extends StatelessWidget {
  const _StepButton({
    required this.icon,
    required this.size,
    required this.enabled,
    required this.onTap,
  });

  final IconData icon;
  final double size;
  final bool enabled;
  final VoidCallback onTap;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return InkWell(
      onTap: enabled ? onTap : null,
      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppTheme.radiusSm),
      child: SizedBox(
        width: size,
        height: size,
        child: Icon(
          icon,
          size: size * 0.5,
          color: enabled ? AppTheme.textPrimary : AppTheme.textTertiary,
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

=== lib/api/product_api.dart ===
import '../models/common.dart';
import '../models/product.dart';
import '../utils/json_util.dart';
import 'api_client.dart';

/// 商品、分类、轮播、评价接口。
class ProductApi {
  ProductApi(this.client);

  final ApiClient client;

  /// 商品列表。
  Future<PagedResult<Product>> listProducts({
    int page = 1,
    int pageSize = 20,
    String? keyword,
    int? categoryId,
    int? status,
    int? minPriceCents,
    int? maxPriceCents,
    String? sort,
    bool? onlyInStock,
  }) async {
    final keywordParam =
        (keyword == null || keyword.isEmpty) ? null : keyword;
    final sortParam = (sort == null || sort.isEmpty) ? null : sort;
    final data = await client.get('/products', query: {
      'page.page': page,
      'page.page_size': pageSize,
      'keyword': ?keywordParam,
      'category_id': ?categoryId,
      'status': ?status,
      'min_price_cents': ?minPriceCents,
      'max_price_cents': ?maxPriceCents,
      'sort': ?sortParam,
      'only_in_stock': ?onlyInStock,
    });
    return PagedResult<Product>(
      items: JsonUtil.asMapList(data['products']).map(Product.fromJson).toList(),
      meta: PageMeta.fromJson(JsonUtil.asMap(data['meta'])),
    );
  }

  /// 商品详情。
  Future<ProductDetail> productDetail(int id) async {
    final data = await client.get('/products/$id');
    return ProductDetail.fromJson(data);
  }

  /// 分类列表。
  Future<List<Category>> categories({int parentId = 0, bool tree = true}) async {
    final data = await client.get('/categories', query: {
      'parent_id': parentId,
      'tree': tree,
    });
    return JsonUtil.asMapList(data['categories']).map(Category.fromJson).toList();
  }

  /// 轮播图列表。
  Future<List<Banner>> banners({String position = 'home_banner'}) async {
    final data = await client.get('/banners', query: {'position': position});
    return JsonUtil.asMapList(data['banners']).map(Banner.fromJson).toList();
  }

  /// 评价列表与汇总。
  Future<(List<Review>, ReviewSummary, PageMeta)> reviews({
    int? productId,
    int page = 1,
    int pageSize = 20,
  }) async {
    final data = await client.get('/reviews', query: {
      'product_id': ?productId,
      'page.page': page,
      'page.page_size': pageSize,
    });
    final list = JsonUtil.asMapList(data['reviews']).map(Review.fromJson).toList();
    final summary = ReviewSummary.fromJson(JsonUtil.asMap(data['summary']));
    final meta = PageMeta.fromJson(JsonUtil.asMap(data['meta']));
    return (list, summary, meta);
  }

  /// 发表评价。
  Future<Review> createReview({
    required int productId,
    required int skuId,
    required int rating,
    required String content,
    List<String> images = const <String>[],
  }) async {
    final data = await client.post('/reviews', body: {
      'product_id': productId,
      'sku_id': skuId,
      'rating': rating,
      'content': content,
      'images': images,
    });
    return Review.fromJson(data);
  }
}
=== lib/api/marketing_api.dart ===
import '../models/marketing.dart';
import 'api_client.dart';
import '../utils/json_util.dart';

/// 营销（优惠券、活动）接口。
class MarketingApi {
  MarketingApi(this.client);

  final ApiClient client;

  /// 我的优惠券。
  ///
  /// 后端 ListUserCoupons 不会从令牌推导用户，需要显式传入 user_id。
  Future<List<UserCoupon>> userCoupons({int userId = 0, int? status}) async {
    final data = await client.get('/users/coupons', query: {
      'user_id': userId,
      'status': ?status,
      'page.page': 1,
      'page.page_size': 100,
    });
    return JsonUtil.asMapList(data['coupons']).map(UserCoupon.fromJson).toList();
  }

  /// 领取优惠券。
  ///
  /// 后端 ReceiveCoupon 同样需要显式传入 user_id。
  Future<UserCoupon> receiveCoupon({
    required int couponId,
    required int userId,
  }) async {
    final data = await client.post('/coupons/$couponId/receive', body: {
      'user_id': userId,
    });
    return UserCoupon.fromJson(data);
  }

  /// 可领取的优惠券。
  Future<List<Coupon>> availableCoupons() async {
    final data = await client.get('/coupons', query: {
      'available': true,
      'page.page': 1,
      'page.page_size': 100,
    });
    return JsonUtil.asMapList(data['coupons']).map(Coupon.fromJson).toList();
  }

  /// 促销活动。
  Future<List<Promotion>> promotions({int? status}) async {
    final data = await client.get('/promotions', query: {
      'status': ?status,
      'page.page': 1,
      'page.page_size': 100,
    });
    return JsonUtil.asMapList(data['promotions']).map(Promotion.fromJson).toList();
  }

  /// 结算预览。
  Future<CheckoutPreview> previewCheckout({
    List<Map<String, dynamic>> items = const <Map<String, dynamic>>[],
    int addressId = 0,
    int couponId = 0,
    int pointsUsed = 0,
  }) async {
    final data = await client.post('/marketing/preview', body: {
      'items': items,
      'address_id': addressId,
      'coupon_id': couponId,
      'points_used': pointsUsed,
    });
    return CheckoutPreview.fromJson(data);
  }
}
=== lib/api/order_api.dart ===
import '../models/order.dart';
import 'api_client.dart';

/// 订单接口。
class OrderApi {
  OrderApi(this.client);

  final ApiClient client;

  /// 创建订单。
  ///
  /// [items] 为直接下单条目，[cartItemIds] 为从购物车结算的条目。
  Future<Order> createOrder({
    List<Map<String, dynamic>> items = const <Map<String, dynamic>>[],
    bool fromCart = false,
    List<int> cartItemIds = const <int>[],
    int addressId = 0,
    int couponId = 0,
    String remark = '',
    String paymentMethod = 'wechat',
    int pointsUsed = 0,
  }) async {
    final data = await client.post('/orders', body: {
      'items': items,
      'from_cart': fromCart,
      'cart_item_ids': cartItemIds,
      'address_id': addressId,
      'coupon_id': couponId,
      'remark': remark,
      'payment_method': paymentMethod,
      'points_used': pointsUsed,
    });
    return Order.fromJson(data);
  }

  /// 订单详情。
  Future<OrderDetail> orderDetail(int id) async {
    final data = await client.get('/orders/$id');
    return OrderDetail.fromJson(data);
  }

  /// 订单列表。
  Future<OrderListResult> listOrders({
    int? status,
    int page = 1,
    int pageSize = 20,
    String? keyword,
  }) async {
    final keywordParam =
        (keyword == null || keyword.isEmpty) ? null : keyword;
    final data = await client.get('/orders', query: {
      'status': ?status,
      'page.page': page,
      'page.page_size': pageSize,
      'keyword': ?keywordParam,
    });
    return OrderListResult.fromJson(data);
  }

  /// 取消订单。
  Future<Order> cancelOrder(int id, {String reason = ''}) async {
    final data = await client.post('/orders/$id/cancel', body: {'reason': reason});
    return Order.fromJson(data);
  }

  /// 确认收货。
  Future<Order> confirmReceipt(int id) async {
    final data = await client.post('/orders/$id/confirm');
    return Order.fromJson(data);
  }

  /// 申请退款。
  Future<dynamic> applyRefund(
    int id, {
    List<int> itemIds = const <int>[],
    int amountCents = 0,
    String reason = '',
    String description = '',
  }) async {
    final data = await client.post('/orders/$id/refund', body: {
      'item_ids': itemIds,
      'amount_cents': amountCents,
      'reason': reason,
      'description': description,
    });
    return data;
  }

  /// 退款原因选项（与前端表单一致）。

  /// 订单状态日志。
  Future<List<OrderStatusLog>> logs(int orderId) async {
    final data = await client.get('/orders/$orderId/logs');
    return JsonUtilLogs.fromJson(data);
  }

  /// 订单数量统计。
  Future<OrderCount> count() async {
    final data = await client.get('/orders/count');
    return OrderCount.fromJson(data);
  }
}

/// 订单日志解析辅助（单独放置以避免与模型文件循环依赖）。
class JsonUtilLogs {
  JsonUtilLogs._();

  static List<OrderStatusLog> fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> data) {
    final raw = data['logs'];
    if (raw is! List) return const <OrderStatusLog>[];
    return raw
        .map((e) => OrderStatusLog.fromJson(
            e is Map ? e.map((k, v) => MapEntry('$k', v)) : <String, dynamic>{}))
        .toList();
  }
}

);

);

final notificationApiProvider = Provider<NotificationApi>(

);

      id: 1,
      orderId: orderId,
      fromStatus: 0,
      toStatus: OrderStatus.pendingPayment.code,
      action: 'create',
      operator: '用户',
      remark: '订单创建成功',
      createdAt: order.createdAt,
    ));
    if (order.paidAt.isNotEmpty) {
      logs.add(OrderStatusLog(
        id: 2,
        orderId: orderId,
        fromStatus: OrderStatus.pendingPayment.code,
        toStatus: OrderStatus.paid.code,
        action: 'pay',
        operator: '用户',
        remark: '用户完成支付',
        createdAt: order.paidAt,
      ));
    }
    if (order.shippedAt.isNotEmpty) {
      logs.add(OrderStatusLog(
        id: 3,
        orderId: orderId,
        fromStatus: OrderStatus.paid.code,
        toStatus: OrderStatus.shipped.code,
        action: 'ship',
        operator: '商家',
        remark: '商品已发出',
        createdAt: order.shippedAt,
      ));
    }
    if (order.completedAt.isNotEmpty) {
      logs.add(OrderStatusLog(
        id: 4,
        orderId: orderId,
        fromStatus: OrderStatus.shipped.code,
        toStatus: OrderStatus.completed.code,
        action: 'confirm',
        operator: '用户',
        remark: '用户确认收货',
        createdAt: order.completedAt,
      ));
    }
    if (order.status == OrderStatus.cancelled) {
      logs.add(OrderStatusLog(
        id: 5,
        orderId: orderId,
        fromStatus: OrderStatus.pendingPayment.code,
        toStatus: OrderStatus.cancelled.code,
        action: 'cancel',
        operator: '用户',
        remark: '用户取消订单',
        createdAt: order.cancelledAt.isEmpty ? _now() : order.cancelledAt,
      ));
    }
    if (order.status == OrderStatus.refunding) {
      logs.add(OrderStatusLog(
        id: 6,
        orderId: orderId,
        fromStatus: OrderStatus.paid.code,
        toStatus: OrderStatus.refunding.code,
        action: 'refund',
        operator: '用户',
        remark: '用户申请退款，等待审核',
        createdAt: _now(),
      ));
    }
    return logs.reversed.toList();
  }

  /// 订单数量统计。
  OrderCount orderCount() {
    final list = orders();
    int count(OrderStatus status) =>
        list.where((order) => order.status == status).length;
    return OrderCount(
      pendingPayment: count(OrderStatus.pendingPayment),
      pendingShipment: count(OrderStatus.paid),
      pendingReceipt: count(OrderStatus.shipped),
      refunding: count(OrderStatus.refunding),
    );
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- 支付

  /// 创建支付单。
  Payment createPayment({required int orderId, required int channel}) {
    final order = orderById(orderId);
    final id = _paymentIdSeq++;
    final payment = Payment(
      id: id,
      paymentNo: 'PAY${DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch}',
      orderId: orderId,
      orderNo: order?.orderNo ?? '',
      userId: MockData.demoUser.id,
      amountCents: order?.payableCents ?? 0,
      status: PaymentStatus.pending,
      channel: PaymentChannel.values.firstWhere(
        (c) => c.code == channel,
        orElse: () => PaymentChannel.wechat,
      ),
      prepayId: 'prepay_${DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch}',
      createdAt: _now(),
    );
    _payments[id] = payment;
    _paymentPolls[id] = 0;
    return payment;
  }

  /// 查询支付状态：轮询两次后返回成功，模拟第三方回调。
  Payment queryPayment({int? paymentId, int? orderId}) {
    Payment? payment;
    if (paymentId != null) {
      payment = _payments[paymentId];
    }
    if (payment == null && orderId != null) {
      for (final item in _payments.values) {
        if (item.orderId == orderId) {
          payment = item;
          break;
        }
      }
    }
    if (payment == null) {
      final order = orderById(orderId ?? 0);
      return Payment(
        id: 0,
        orderId: orderId ?? 0,
        orderNo: order?.orderNo ?? '',
        amountCents: order?.payableCents ?? 0,
        status: PaymentStatus.pending,
        channel: PaymentChannel.wechat,
        createdAt: _now(),
      );
    }
    final polls = (_paymentPolls[payment.id] ?? 0) + 1;
    _paymentPolls[payment.id] = polls;
    if (polls >= 2) {
      final updated = Payment(
        id: payment.id,
        paymentNo: payment.paymentNo,
        orderId: payment.orderId,
        orderNo: payment.orderNo,
        userId: payment.userId,
        amountCents: payment.amountCents,
        refundedCents: payment.refundedCents,
        status: PaymentStatus.success,
        channel: payment.channel,
        transactionId: 'TXN${DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch}',
        prepayId: payment.prepayId,
        payParams: payment.payParams,
        failReason: payment.failReason,
        paidAt: _now(),
        createdAt: payment.createdAt,
        updatedAt: _now(),
      );
      _payments[payment.id] = updated;
      _markOrderPaid(payment.orderId);
      return updated;
    }
    return payment;
  }

  void _markOrderPaid(int orderId) {
    final index = _orders.indexWhere((order) => order.id == orderId);
    if (index < 0) return;
    final order = _orders[index];
    _orders[index] = Order(
      id: order.id,
      orderNo: order.orderNo,
      userId: order.userId,
      userNickname: order.userNickname,
      status: OrderStatus.paid,
      totalAmountCents: order.totalAmountCents,
      discountCents: order.discountCents,
      freightCents: order.freightCents,
      payableCents: order.payableCents,
      paidCents: order.payableCents,
      refundedCents: order.refundedCents,
      itemCount: order.itemCount,
      totalQuantity: order.totalQuantity,
      address: order.address,
      remark: order.remark,
      paymentMethod: order.paymentMethod,
      paidAt: _now(),
      shippedAt: order.shippedAt,
      completedAt: order.completedAt,
      cancelledAt: order.cancelledAt,
      createdAt: order.createdAt,
      updatedAt: _now(),
      adminRemark: order.adminRemark,
      shipmentId: order.shipmentId,
    );
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- 用户

  List<Address> addresses() =>
      _addresses.isEmpty ? List<Address>.of(MockData.addresses) : _addresses;

  Address saveAddress({
    int id = 0,
    required String receiver,
    required String phone,
    required String province,
    required String city,
    required String district,
    required String detail,
    String postalCode = '',
    String tag = '',
    bool isDefault = false,
  }) {
    if (_addresses.isEmpty) {
      _addresses.addAll(MockData.addresses);
    }
    final address = Address(
      id: id == 0 ? _addressIdSeq++ : id,
      receiver: receiver,
      phone: phone,
      province: province,
      city: city,
      district: district,
      detail: detail,
      postalCode: postalCode,
      tag: tag,
      isDefault: isDefault,
    );
    final index = _addresses.indexWhere((a) => a.id == id);
    if (index >= 0) {
      _addresses[index] = address;
    } else {
      _addresses.add(address);
    }
    if (isDefault) {
      for (var i = 0; i < _addresses.length; i++) {
        if (_addresses[i].id != address.id) {
          _addresses[i] = _addresses[i].copyWith(isDefault: false);
        }
      }
    }
    return address;
  }

  void deleteAddress(int id) =>
      _addresses.removeWhere((address) => address.id == id);

  void setDefaultAddress(int id) {
    for (var i = 0; i < _addresses.length; i++) {
      _addresses[i] = _addresses[i].copyWith(isDefault: _addresses[i].id == id);
    }
  }

  /// 收藏列表。
  List<FavoriteProduct> favorites() => MockData.products
      .where((product) => _favoriteIds.contains(product.id))
      .map((product) => FavoriteProduct(
            id: product.id,
            productId: product.id,
            title: product.title,
            cover: product.cover,
            priceCents: product.minPriceCents,
            createdAt: product.createdAt,
          ))
      .toList();

  /// 收藏/取消收藏。
  bool toggleFavorite(int productId) {
    if (_favoriteIds.contains(productId)) {
      _favoriteIds.remove(productId);
      return false;
    }
    _favoriteIds.add(productId);
    return true;
  }

  bool isFavorite(int productId) => _favoriteIds.contains(productId);

  /// 我的优惠券。
  List<UserCoupon> userCoupons({int? status}) {
    if (status == null) return List<UserCoupon>.of(_userCoupons);
    return _userCoupons
        .where((coupon) => coupon.status.code == status)
        .toList(growable: false);
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- 积分

  /// 积分明细。
  List<PointsLog> pointsLogs() => List<PointsLog>.unmodifiable(_pointsLogs);

  /// 当前积分。
  int currentPoints() => MockData.demoCurrentPoints;

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- 发票

  /// 发票申请列表。
  List<Invoice> invoices() => List<Invoice>.unmodifiable(_invoices);

  /// 申请开票。
  Invoice createInvoice({
    required String orderNo,
    required String title,
    String taxNo = '',
    String type = 'personal',
    String email = '',
    int amountCents = 0,
    String remark = '',
  }) {
    final invoice = Invoice(
      id: _addressIdSeq++,
      userId: MockData.demoUser.id,
      orderNo: orderNo,
      title: title,
      taxNo: taxNo,
      type: type,
      email: email,
      amountCents: amountCents,
      status: 0,
      invoiceUrl: '',
      remark: remark,
      createdAt: _now(),
    );
    _invoices.insert(0, invoice);
    return invoice;
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- 领券中心

  /// 可领取的优惠券。
  List<Coupon> couponCenterCoupons() =>
      List<Coupon>.unmodifiable(MockData.couponCenterCoupons);

  /// 已领取的优惠券 id。
  Set<int> receivedCouponIds() => Set<int>.unmodifiable(_receivedCouponIds);

  /// 领取优惠券，已领取时返回 null。
  UserCoupon? receiveCoupon(int couponId) {
    if (_receivedCouponIds.contains(couponId)) return null;
    _receivedCouponIds.add(couponId);
    Coupon? target;
    for (final coupon in MockData.couponCenterCoupons) {
      if (coupon.id == couponId) {
        target = coupon;
        break;
      }
    }
    if (target == null) return null;
    final userCoupon = UserCoupon(
      id: 900 + couponId,
      userId: MockData.demoUser.id,
      couponId: couponId,
      coupon: target,
      status: UserCouponStatus.unused,
      receivedAt: _now(),
    );
    _userCoupons.insert(0, userCoupon);
    return userCoupon;
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- 消息

  List<AppNotification> notifications() {
    if (_notifications.isEmpty) {
      _notifications.addAll(MockData.notifications);
    }
    return List<AppNotification>.of(_notifications);
  }

  void markRead(int id) {
    final index = _notifications.indexWhere((n) => n.id == id);
    if (index < 0) return;
    final item = _notifications[index];
    _notifications[index] = AppNotification(
      id: item.id,
      userId: item.userId,
      type: item.type,
      title: item.title,
      content: item.content,
      link: item.link,
      bizId: item.bizId,
      read: true,
      createdAt: item.createdAt,
      readAt: _now(),
    );
  }

  void markAllRead() {
    for (var i = 0; i < _notifications.length; i++) {
      final item = _notifications[i];
      if (item.read) continue;
      _notifications[i] = AppNotification(
        id: item.id,
        userId: item.userId,
        type: item.type,
        title: item.title,
        content: item.content,
        link: item.link,
        bizId: item.bizId,
        read: true,
        createdAt: item.createdAt,
        readAt: _now(),
      );
    }
  }

  int unreadCount() =>
      notifications().where((n) => !n.read).length;

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- 物流

  /// 订单物流。
  LogisticsResult orderLogistics(int orderId) => LogisticsResult(
        shipment: MockData.demoShipment(orderId),
        tracks: MockData.demoTracks(orderId),
      );

  String _now() => DateTime.now().toIso8601String();
}

/// 订单列表页。
class OrderListPage extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const OrderListPage({super.key});

  @override
  ConsumerState<OrderListPage> createState() => _OrderListPageState();
}

class _OrderListPageState extends ConsumerState<OrderListPage> {
  static final List<({String label, int? status})> _tabs = [
    (label: '全部', status: null),
    (label: '待付款', status: OrderStatus.pendingPayment.code),
    (label: '待发货', status: OrderStatus.paid.code),
    (label: '待收货', status: OrderStatus.shipped.code),
    (label: '退款', status: OrderStatus.refunding.code),
  ];

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return DefaultTabController(
      length: _tabs.length,
      child: Scaffold(
        appBar: AppBar(
          title: const Text('我的订单'),
          bottom: TabBar(
            isScrollable: true,
            tabAlignment: TabAlignment.start,
            tabs: _tabs
                .map((tab) => Tab(text: tab.label))
                .toList(),
          ),
        ),
        body: Column(
          children: [
            const DemoBanner(),
            Expanded(
              child: TabBarView(
                children: _tabs
                    .map((tab) => _OrderListView(status: tab.status))
                    .toList(),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// 单个 Tab 的订单列表。
class _OrderListView extends ConsumerWidget {
  const _OrderListView({required this.status});

  final int? status;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) {
    final orders = ref.watch(orderListProvider(status));
    return orders.when(
      data: (result) {
        if (result.orders.isEmpty) {
          return const EmptyState(
            title: '暂无相关订单',
            subtitle: '去首页挑选心仪商品吧',
            icon: Icons.receipt_long_outlined,
          );
        }
        return RefreshIndicator(
          color: AppTheme.brand,
          onRefresh: () async => ref.invalidate(orderListProvider(status)),
          child: ListView.separated(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(12, 12, 12, 16),
            itemCount: result.orders.length,
            separatorBuilder: (context, index) => const SizedBox(height: 10),
            itemBuilder: (context, index) => _OrderCard(order: result.orders[index]),
          ),
        );
      },
      loading: () => const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()),
      error: (error, stack) => const EmptyState(
        title: '订单加载失败',
        subtitle: '请下拉重试',
        icon: Icons.wifi_off_rounded,
      ),
    );
  }
}

    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
      SnackBar(content: Text(error ?? '订单已取消')),
    );
  }

  Future<void> _confirmLogout(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) async {
    final confirmed = await showDialog<bool>(

            child: const Text('退出'),

        ],

    );
    if (confirmed != true) return;
    await ref.read(authProvider.notifier).logout();

          ],
        ),
        error: (error, stack) => const Text(
          '用户信息加载失败',
          style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 13),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _metric(BuildContext context, String label, String value) {
    return Expanded(
      child: Column(
        children: [
          Text(
            value,
            style: const TextStyle(
              fontSize: 17,
              fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
              color: Colors.white,
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 2),
          Text(
            label,
            style: TextStyle(
              fontSize: 12,
              color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.8),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// 订单状态入口。
class _OrderEntryCard extends ConsumerWidget {
  const _OrderEntryCard({required this.counts});

  final AsyncValue<OrderCount> counts;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) {
    final count = counts.asData?.value;
    final pendingPayment = count?.pendingPayment ?? 0;
    final pendingShipment = count?.pendingShipment ?? 0;
    final pendingReceipt = count?.pendingReceipt ?? 0;
    final refunding = count?.refunding ?? 0;

    final entries = <({String label, IconData icon, int badge})>[
      (label: '待付款', icon: Icons.account_balance_wallet_outlined, badge: pendingPayment),
      (label: '待发货', icon: Icons.inventory_2_outlined, badge: pendingShipment),
      (label: '待收货', icon: Icons.local_shipping_outlined, badge: pendingReceipt),
      (label: '退款/售后', icon: Icons.assignment_return_outlined, badge: refunding),
    ];

    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 16, horizontal: 8),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: AppTheme.bgCard,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppTheme.radiusLg),
        boxShadow: AppTheme.shadowSm,
      ),
      child: Column(
        children: [
          Row(
            children: [
              Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.only(left: 8),
                child: Text(
                  '我的订单',
                  style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleMedium,
                ),
              ),
              const Spacer(),
              GestureDetector(
                onTap: () => context.push(AppRoutes.orders),
                child: const Row(
                  children: [
                    Text(
                      '全部订单',
                      style: TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 12, color: AppTheme.textTertiary),
                    ),
                    Icon(Icons.chevron_right_rounded,
                        size: 16, color: AppTheme.textTertiary),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 16),
          Row(
            children: [
              for (final entry in entries)
                Expanded(
                  child: _OrderEntry(
                    label: entry.label,
                    icon: entry.icon,
                    badge: entry.badge,
                    onTap: () => context.push(AppRoutes.orders),
                  ),
                ),
            ],
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _OrderEntry extends StatelessWidget {
  const _OrderEntry({
    required this.label,
    required this.icon,
    required this.badge,
    required this.onTap,
  });

  final String label;
  final IconData icon;
  final int badge;
  final VoidCallback onTap;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return GestureDetector(
      onTap: onTap,
      behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
      child: Column(
        children: [
          Badge(
            isLabelVisible: badge > 0,
            label: Text('$badge'),
            child: Icon(icon, size: 24, color: AppTheme.textSecondary),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 6),
          Text(
            label,
            style: const TextStyle(
                fontSize: 12, color: AppTheme.textSecondary),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';

import '../mock/mock_data.dart';
import '../mock/mock_store.dart';
import '../models/common.dart';
import '../models/product.dart';
import 'core_providers.dart';

/// 商品分类（含二级）。
final categoriesProvider = FutureProvider<List<Category>>((ref) async {
  try {
    return await ref.read(productApiProvider).categories();
  } catch (_) {
    ref.read(demoModeProvider.notifier).enable();
    return <Category>[...MockData.topCategories, ...MockData.subCategories];
  }
});

/// 一级分类。
final topCategoriesProvider = FutureProvider<List<Category>>((ref) async {
  final all = await ref.watch(categoriesProvider.future);
  return all.where((category) => category.parentId == 0).toList();
});

/// 轮播图。
final bannersProvider = FutureProvider<List<Banner>>((ref) async {
  try {
    return await ref.read(productApiProvider).banners();
  } catch (_) {
    ref.read(demoModeProvider.notifier).enable();
    return MockData.banners;
  }
});

/// 首页推荐商品。
final recommendProductsProvider = FutureProvider<PagedResult<Product>>(
    (ref) async {
  try {
    return await ref
        .read(productApiProvider)
        .listProducts(page: 1, pageSize: 10, sort: 'sales');
  } catch (_) {
    ref.read(demoModeProvider.notifier).enable();
    final list = MockStore.instance.products(sort: 'sales');
    return PagedResult<Product>(
      items: list,
      meta: PageMeta(page: 1, pageSize: list.length, total: list.length, totalPages: 1),
    );
  }
});

/// 秒杀 / 促销商品。
final flashSaleProductsProvider = FutureProvider<List<Product>>((ref) async {
  try {
    final result = await ref
        .read(productApiProvider)
        .listProducts(page: 1, pageSize: 6, sort: 'newest');
    return result.items;
  } catch (_) {
    ref.read(demoModeProvider.notifier).enable();
    return MockStore.instance.products(sort: 'newest').take(6).toList();
  }
});

/// 商品详情。
final productDetailProvider =
    FutureProvider.family<ProductDetail, int>((ref, id) async {
  try {
    return await ref.read(productApiProvider).productDetail(id);
  } catch (_) {
    ref.read(demoModeProvider.notifier).enable();
    return MockStore.instance.productDetail(id);
  }
});

/// 评价数据（列表 + 汇总）。
class ProductReviewsData {
  const ProductReviewsData(this.reviews, this.summary);

  final List<Review> reviews;
  final ReviewSummary summary;
}

final productReviewsProvider =
    FutureProvider.family<ProductReviewsData, int>((ref, productId) async {
  try {
    final result = await ref
        .read(productApiProvider)
        .reviews(productId: productId, pageSize: 20);
    return ProductReviewsData(result.$1, result.$2);
  } catch (_) {
    ref.read(demoModeProvider.notifier).enable();
    final reviews =
        MockData.reviews.where((r) => r.productId == productId).toList();
    final good = reviews.where((r) => r.rating >= 4).length;
    final medium = reviews.where((r) => r.rating == 3).length;
    final bad = reviews.where((r) => r.rating <= 2).length;
    final average = reviews.isEmpty
        ? 0.0
        : reviews.map((r) => r.rating).reduce((a, b) => a + b) /
            reviews.length;
    return ProductReviewsData(
      reviews,
      ReviewSummary(
        total: reviews.length,
        good: good,
        medium: medium,
        bad: bad,
        average: average,
      ),
    );
  }
});

/// 商品筛选条件（使用 record 作为 family key，结构化相等）。
typedef ProductFilter = ({
  int? categoryId,
  String sort,
  int? minPriceCents,
  int? maxPriceCents,
});

/// 按条件筛选商品列表。
final filteredProductsProvider =
    FutureProvider.family<PagedResult<Product>, ProductFilter>((ref, filter) async {
  try {
    return await ref.read(productApiProvider).listProducts(
          categoryId: filter.categoryId,
          sort: filter.sort,
          minPriceCents: filter.minPriceCents,
          maxPriceCents: filter.maxPriceCents,
          pageSize: 50,
        );
  } catch (_) {
    ref.read(demoModeProvider.notifier).enable();
    final list = MockStore.instance.products(
      categoryId: filter.categoryId,
      sort: filter.sort,
      minPriceCents: filter.minPriceCents,
      maxPriceCents: filter.maxPriceCents,
    );
    return PagedResult<Product>(
      items: list,
      meta: PageMeta(page: 1, pageSize: list.length, total: list.length, totalPages: 1),
    );
  }
});

/// 搜索结果。
final searchProductsProvider =
    FutureProvider.family<PagedResult<Product>, String>((ref, keyword) async {
  try {
    return await ref
        .read(productApiProvider)
        .listProducts(keyword: keyword, pageSize: 30);
  } catch (_) {
    ref.read(demoModeProvider.notifier).enable();
    final list = MockStore.instance.products(keyword: keyword);
    return PagedResult<Product>(
      items: list,
      meta: PageMeta(page: 1, pageSize: list.length, total: list.length, totalPages: 1),
    );
  }
});

import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';

import '../api/api_client.dart';
import '../mock/mock_data.dart';
import '../models/user.dart';
import 'core_providers.dart';

/// 登录态。
class AuthState {
  const AuthState({
    this.bootstrapping = true,
    this.authenticated = false,
    this.user,
    this.loading = false,
    this.error,
  });

  /// 是否仍在读取本地令牌。
  final bool bootstrapping;

  /// 是否已登录。
  final bool authenticated;

  /// 当前用户。
  final User? user;

  /// 登录 / 注册请求中。
  final bool loading;

  /// 错误提示。
  final String? error;

  AuthState copyWith({
    bool? bootstrapping,
    bool? authenticated,
    User? user,
    bool? loading,
    String? error,
    bool clearError = false,
  }) =>
      AuthState(
        bootstrapping: bootstrapping ?? this.bootstrapping,
        authenticated: authenticated ?? this.authenticated,
        user: user ?? this.user,
        loading: loading ?? this.loading,
        error: clearError ? null : (error ?? this.error),
      );
}

class AuthNotifier extends Notifier<AuthState> {
  @override
  AuthState build() => const AuthState(bootstrapping: true);

  /// 启动时读取本地令牌，决定进入首页还是登录页。
  Future<void> bootstrap() async {
    final store = ref.read(tokenStoreProvider);
    if (!store.hasSession) {
      _set(const AuthState(bootstrapping: false, authenticated: false));
      return;
    }
    try {
      final user = await ref.read(authApiProvider).profile();
      await store.saveUser(user);
      _set(AuthState(bootstrapping: false, authenticated: true, user: user));
    } on NetworkException {
      // 后端未就绪：使用本地缓存用户进入演示模式。
      ref.read(demoModeProvider.notifier).enable();
      final cached = store.cachedUser ?? MockData.demoUser;
      _set(AuthState(bootstrapping: false, authenticated: true, user: cached));
    } catch (_) {
      await store.clear();
      _set(const AuthState(bootstrapping: false, authenticated: false));
    }
  }

  /// 账号密码登录。
  Future<bool> login(String username, String password) async {
    _set(state.copyWith(loading: true, clearError: true));
    try {
      final session = await ref.read(authApiProvider).login(username, password);

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import 'app_tokens.dart';
