/// App 主题：完全基于 design-tokens 的 AppColors / AppBreakpoints 构建。
///
/// 颜色与尺寸来自 tool/sync_tokens.sh 同步生成的常量（唯一来源仍是 design-tokens）。
class AppTheme {
  AppTheme._();

  // 常用颜色（来自令牌）。
  static const Color brand = AppColorTokens.colorBrand600;
  static const Color brandDark = AppColorTokens.colorBrand700;
  static const Color brand50 = AppColorTokens.colorBrand50;
  static const Color brand100 = AppColorTokens.colorBrand100;
  static const Color brand200 = AppColorTokens.colorBrand200;
  static const Color brand900 = AppColorTokens.colorBrand900;
  static const Color brand600 = AppColorTokens.colorBrand600;
  static const Color accent = AppColorTokens.colorAccent500;
  static const Color accent500 = AppColorTokens.colorAccent500;
  static const Color accent50 = AppColorTokens.colorAccent50;
  static const Color accent600 = AppColorTokens.colorAccent600;
  static const Color accent700 = AppColorTokens.colorAccent700;
  static const Color success = AppColorTokens.colorSuccess500;
  static const Color success600 = AppColorTokens.colorSuccess600;
  static const Color success50 = AppColorTokens.colorSuccess50;
  static const Color success700 = AppColorTokens.colorSuccess700;
  static const Color warning = AppColorTokens.colorWarning500;
  static const Color warning600 = AppColorTokens.colorWarning600;
  static const Color warning50 = AppColorTokens.colorWarning50;
  static const Color warning700 = AppColorTokens.colorWarning700;
  static const Color danger = AppColorTokens.colorDanger500;
  static const Color danger600 = AppColorTokens.colorDanger600;
  static const Color danger50 = AppColorTokens.colorDanger50;
  static const Color danger700 = AppColorTokens.colorDanger700;
  static const Color info = AppColorTokens.colorInfo500;
  static const Color info600 = AppColorTokens.colorInfo600;
  static const Color info50 = AppColorTokens.colorInfo50;
  static const Color info700 = AppColorTokens.colorInfo700;
  static const Color purple500 = AppColorTokens.colorPurple500;
  static const Color purple50 = AppColorTokens.colorPurple50;
  static const Color textPrimary = AppColorTokens.colorTextPrimary;
  static const Color textSecondary = AppColorTokens.colorTextSecondary;
  static const Color textTertiary = AppColorTokens.colorTextTertiary;
  static const Color textQuaternary = AppColorTokens.colorTextQuaternary;
  static const Color textPlaceholder = AppColorTokens.colorTextPlaceholder;
  static const Color bgPage = AppColorTokens.colorBgPage;
  static const Color bgCard = AppColorTokens.colorBgCard;

  static const Color bgMuted = AppColorTokens.colorBgMuted;
  static const Color borderSubtle = AppColorTokens.colorBorderSubtle;
  static const Color borderDefault = AppColorTokens.colorBorderDefault;
  static const Color borderStrong = AppColorTokens.colorBorderStrong;

  // 圆角（来自令牌）。
  static const double radiusXs = AppSizeTokens.radiusXs;
  static const double radiusSm = AppSizeTokens.radiusSm;
  static const double radiusMd = AppSizeTokens.radiusMd;
  static const double radiusLg = AppSizeTokens.radiusLg;
  static const double radiusXl = AppSizeTokens.radiusXl;

  /// 浅色主题。
  static ThemeData light() {
    final colorScheme = ColorScheme.fromSeed(
      seedColor: brand,
      brightness: Brightness.light,
    ).copyWith(

      onPrimary: Colors.white,

          borderSide: BorderSide.none,
        ),
        focusedBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(radiusMd),
          borderSide: const BorderSide(color: brand, width: 1.4),
        ),
        errorBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(radiusMd),
          borderSide: const BorderSide(color: danger),
        ),
        focusedErrorBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(radiusMd),
          borderSide: const BorderSide(color: danger, width: 1.4),
        ),
      ),
      navigationBarTheme: NavigationBarThemeData(
        backgroundColor: bgCard,
        indicatorColor: brand50,
        elevation: 0,
        height: 58,
        labelTextStyle: WidgetStateProperty.resolveWith(
          (states) => TextStyle(
            fontSize: 11,
            fontWeight: states.contains(WidgetState.selected)
                ? FontWeight.w600
                : FontWeight.w400,
            color: states.contains(WidgetState.selected) ? brand : textTertiary,
          ),
        ),
      ),
      tabBarTheme: const TabBarThemeData(
        labelColor: brand,
        unselectedLabelColor: textSecondary,
        indicatorSize: TabBarIndicatorSize.label,
        dividerColor: Colors.transparent,
        labelStyle: TextStyle(fontSize: 14, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
        unselectedLabelStyle: TextStyle(fontSize: 14),
      ),
      bottomSheetTheme: const BottomSheetThemeData(
        backgroundColor: bgCard,
        surfaceTintColor: Colors.transparent,
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(radiusXl)),
        ),
      ),
      snackBarTheme: SnackBarThemeData(
        behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
        backgroundColor: textPrimary,
        contentTextStyle: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 14),
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(radiusMd),
        ),
      ),
      textTheme: const TextTheme(
        titleLarge: TextStyle(
            fontSize: 20, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600, color: textPrimary),
        titleMedium: TextStyle(
            fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600, color: textPrimary),
        titleSmall: TextStyle(
            fontSize: 15, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600, color: textPrimary),
        bodyLarge: TextStyle(fontSize: 15, color: textPrimary, height: 1.5),
        bodyMedium: TextStyle(fontSize: 14, color: textSecondary, height: 1.5),
        bodySmall: TextStyle(fontSize: 13, color: textSecondary, height: 1.5),
        labelSmall: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: textTertiary),
      ),
    );
  }
}

1→#!/usr/bin/env node
     2→/**
     3→ * 生成 README 的「页面清单」章节。
     4→ *
     5→ * 用法：node scripts/gen-page-list.mjs [--write]
     6→ *
     7→ * 不带 --write 时只打印统计结果；带 --write 时直接改写 README.md 中
     8→ * `<!-- PAGE_LIST_START -->` 与 `<!-- PAGE_LIST_END -->` 之间的内容。
     9→ */
    10→import fs from 'node:fs'
    11→import path from 'node:path'
    12→import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url'
    13→
    14→const root = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..')
    15→const write = process.argv.includes('--write')
    16→
    17→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    18→// 采集各端页面
    19→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    20→
    21→/** 递归列出目录下匹配后缀的文件。 */
    22→function walk(dir, ext) {
    23→  const out = []
    24→  if (!fs.existsSync(dir)) return out
    25→  for (const entry of fs.readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true })) {
    26→    const full = path.join(dir, entry.name)
    27→    if (entry.isDirectory()) {
    28→      out.push(...walk(full, ext))
    29→    } else if (entry.name.endsWith(ext)) {
    30→      out.push(full)
    31→    }
    32→  }
    33→  return out
    34→}
    35→
    36→/** 小程序：统计主包 pages[] 与分包 subPackages[].pages[] 之和。 */
    37→function collectMiniapp() {
    38→  const appJsonPath = path.join(root, 'apps/miniapp/app.json')
    39→  const app = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(appJsonPath, 'utf8'))
    40→  const rows = []
    41→  for (const p of app.pages ?? []) {
    42→    rows.push({ name: path.basename(p), dir: `apps/miniapp/${p}` })
    43→  }
    44→  for (const sp of app.subPackages ?? []) {
    45→    for (const p of sp.pages ?? []) {
    46→      rows.push({
    47→        name: path.basename(p),
    48→        dir: `apps/miniapp/${sp.root}/${p}`,
    49→        pkg: sp.root,
    50→      })
    51→    }
    52→  }
    53→  return rows.sort((a, b) => a.dir.localeCompare(b.dir))
    54→}
    55→
    56→/** Flutter：lib/pages 下的 *_page.dart。 */
    57→function collectFlutter() {
    58→  const dir = path.join(root, 'apps/flutter/lib/pages')
    59→  return walk(dir, '_page.dart')
    60→    .map((f) => ({
    61→      name: path.basename(f, '.dart').replace(/_page$/, ''),
    62→      file: path.relative(root, f),
    63→    }))
    64→    .sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name))
    65→}
    66→
    67→/** Vue3：src/views 下的 *View.vue。 */
    68→function collectVue() {
    69→  const dir = path.join(root, 'apps/web-vue/src/views')
    70→  return walk(dir, 'View.vue')
    71→    .map((f) => ({
    72→      name: path.basename(f, '.vue').replace(/View$/, ''),
    73→      file: path.relative(root, f),
    74→    }))
    75→    .sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name))
    76→}
    77→
    78→/** React：src/pages 下的 *.tsx。 */
    79→function collectReact() {
    80→  const dir = path.join(root, 'apps/admin-react/src/pages')
    81→  return walk(dir, '.tsx')
    82→    .map((f) => ({
    83→      name: path.basename(f, '.tsx'),
    84→      file: path.relative(root, f),
    85→    }))
    86→    .sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name))
    87→}
    88→
    89→/** 统计路由表里的路由条数（Vue3 / React）。 */
    90→function countRoutes(file, pattern) {
    91→  if (!fs.existsSync(file)) return 0
    92→  const src = fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf8')
    93→  const matches = src.match(pattern)
    94→  return matches ? matches.length : 0
    95→}
    96→
    97→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    98→// 渲染
    99→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   100→
   101→function render() {
   102→  const mini = collectMiniapp()
   103→  const flutter = collectFlutter()
   104→  const vue = collectVue()
   105→  const react = collectReact()
   106→
   107→  const vueRoutes = countRoutes(
   108→    path.join(root, 'apps/web-vue/src/router/index.ts'),
   109→    /path:\s*'/g,
   110→  )
   111→  const reactRoutes = countRoutes(
   112→    path.join(root, 'apps/admin-react/src/router/index.tsx'),
   113→    /<Route\s+path=/g,
   114→  )
   115→
   116→  const lines = []
   117→  lines.push('## 页面清单')
   118→  lines.push('')
   119→  lines.push('> 本章节由 `node scripts/gen-page-list.mjs --write` 自动生成，请勿手工编辑。')
   120→  lines.push('')
   121→  lines.push(
   122→    `四端**各自** ${mini.length} / ${flutter.length} / ${vue.length} / ${react.length} 页，` +
   123→      `合计 **${mini.length + flutter.length + vue.length + react.length} 个页面**。`,
   124→  )
   125→  lines.push('')
   126→
   127→  lines.push(`### 微信小程序（${mini.length} 页）`)
   128→  lines.push('')
   129→  lines.push('| 页面 | 目录 |')
   130→  lines.push('| --- | --- |')
   131→  for (const p of mini) {
   132→    lines.push(`| ${p.name}${p.pkg ? ` （${p.pkg} 分包）` : ''} | \`${p.dir}\` |`)
   133→  }
   134→  lines.push('')
   135→
   136→  lines.push(`### Flutter 移动端（${flutter.length} 页）`)
   137→  lines.push('')
   138→  lines.push('| 页面 | 文件 |')
   139→  lines.push('| --- | --- |')
   140→  for (const p of flutter) {
   141→    lines.push(`| ${p.name} | \`${p.file}\` |`)
   142→  }
   143→  lines.push('')
   144→
   145→  lines.push(`### Vue3 Web 用户端（${vue.length} 页 / ${vueRoutes} 条路由）`)
   146→  lines.push('')
   147→  lines.push('| 页面 | 文件 |')
   148→  lines.push('| --- | --- |')
   149→  for (const p of vue) {
   150→    lines.push(`| ${p.name} | \`${p.file}\` |`)
   151→  }
   152→  lines.push('')
   153→
   154→  lines.push(`### React 后台管理（${react.length} 页 / ${reactRoutes} 条路由）`)
   155→  lines.push('')
   156→  lines.push('| 组件 | 文件 |')
   157→  lines.push('| --- | --- |')
   158→  for (const p of react) {
   159→    lines.push(`| ${p.name.replace(/\.tsx$/, '')} | \`${p.file}\` |`)
   160→  }
   161→  lines.push('')
   162→
   163→  return {
   164→    text: lines.join('\n'),
   165→    stats: { mini: mini.length, flutter: flutter.length, vue: vue.length, react: react.length },
   166→  }
   167→}
   168→
   169→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   170→// 入口
   171→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   172→
   173→const { text, stats } = render()
   174→
   175→console.log('页面统计：')
   176→console.log(`  微信小程序 : ${stats.mini}`)
   177→console.log(`  Flutter    : ${stats.flutter}`)
   178→console.log(`  Vue3       : ${stats.vue}`)
   179→console.log(`  React      : ${stats.react}`)
   180→console.log(`  合计       : ${stats.mini + stats.flutter + stats.vue + stats.react}`)
   181→
   182→if (!write) {
   183→  process.exit(0)
   184→}
   185→
   186→const readmePath = path.join(root, 'README.md')
   187→const readme = fs.readFileSync(readmePath, 'utf8')
   188→const startMark = '<!-- PAGE_LIST_START -->'
   189→const endMark = '<!-- PAGE_LIST_END -->'
   190→
   191→const startIdx = readme.indexOf(startMark)
   192→const endIdx = readme.indexOf(endMark)
   193→if (startIdx === -1 || endIdx === -1) {
   194→  console.error('README.md 中找不到 PAGE_LIST_START / PAGE_LIST_END 标记')
   195→  process.exit(1)
   196→}
   197→
   198→const next =
   199→  readme.slice(0, startIdx + startMark.length) +
   200→  '\n\n' +
   201→  text +
   202→  '\n' +
   203→  readme.slice(endIdx)
   204→
   205→fs.writeFileSync(readmePath, next, 'utf8')
   206→console.log('README.md 页面清单章节已更新')
```

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';

import 'package:shopstack_app/api/token_store.dart';
import 'package:shopstack_app/main.dart';
import 'package:shopstack_app/providers/core_providers.dart';

Future<void> boot(WidgetTester tester) async {
  SharedPreferences.setMockInitialValues(<String, Object>{
    'shopstack_access_token': 'test-token',
    'shopstack_refresh_token': 'test-refresh',
  });
  final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
  await tester.pumpWidget(
    ProviderScope(
      overrides: [tokenStoreProvider.overrideWithValue(TokenStore(prefs))],
      child: const ShopStackApp(),
    ),
  );
  await tester.pump();
  await tester.pump(const Duration(seconds: 1));
  await tester.pump(const Duration(seconds: 1));
}

Future<void> openProfileItem(WidgetTester tester, String label) async {
  await tester.tap(find.text('我的'));
  await tester.pump();
  await tester.pump(const Duration(seconds: 1));
  final finder = find.text(label).first;
  await tester.ensureVisible(finder);
  await tester.pump();
  await tester.tap(finder);
  await tester.pump();
  await tester.pump(const Duration(seconds: 1));
}

Future<void> goBack(WidgetTester tester) async {
  await tester.tap(find.byType(BackButton));
  await tester.pump();
  await tester.pump(const Duration(seconds: 1));
}

void main() {
  testWidgets('积分明细页可渲染并展示当前积分', (tester) async {
    await boot(tester);
    await openProfileItem(tester, '积分明细');
    expect(find.text('积分明细'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('当前可用积分'), findsOneWidget);
    // 标题与业务类型标签都会展示「签到奖励」。
    expect(find.text('签到奖励'), findsWidgets);
    expect(tester.takeException(), isNull);
    await goBack(tester);
  });

  testWidgets('发票管理页可渲染', (tester) async {
    await boot(tester);
    await openProfileItem(tester, '发票管理');
    expect(find.text('发票管理'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('申请开票'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(tester.takeException(), isNull);
    await goBack(tester);
  });

  testWidgets('物流跟踪页可渲染', (tester) async {
    await boot(tester);
    await openProfileItem(tester, '物流跟踪');
    expect(find.text('物流跟踪'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(tester.takeException(), isNull);
    await goBack(tester);
  });

  testWidgets('领券中心可领取优惠券且按钮变为已领取', (tester) async {
    await boot(tester);
    await openProfileItem(tester, '领券中心');
    expect(find.text('领券中心'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('立即领取'), findsWidgets);

    await tester.tap(find.text('立即领取').first);
    await tester.pump();
    await tester.pump(const Duration(seconds: 1));
    expect(find.text('已领取'), findsWidgets);
    expect(tester.takeException(), isNull);
    await goBack(tester);
  });

  testWidgets('发表评价页可渲染', (tester) async {
    await boot(tester);
    await openProfileItem(tester, '发表评价');
    expect(find.text('发表评价'), findsWidgets);
    await tester.scrollUntilVisible(
      find.text('评分'),
      220,
      scrollable: find.byType(Scrollable).first,
    );
    expect(find.text('评分'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('评价内容'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(tester.takeException(), isNull);
    await goBack(tester);
  });

  testWidgets('售后申请页可渲染', (tester) async {
    await boot(tester);
    await openProfileItem(tester, '售后申请');
    expect(find.text('售后申请'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('退款原因'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('退款金额'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('提交退款申请'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(tester.takeException(), isNull);
    await goBack(tester);
  });
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import 'package:shopstack_app/api/token_store.dart';
import 'package:shopstack_app/main.dart';
import 'package:shopstack_app/providers/core_providers.dart';

Future<void> boot(WidgetTester tester) async {
  SharedPreferences.setMockInitialValues(<String, Object>{
    'shopstack_access_token': 'test-token',
    'shopstack_refresh_token': 'test-refresh',
  });
  final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
  await tester.pumpWidget(
    ProviderScope(
      overrides: [tokenStoreProvider.overrideWithValue(TokenStore(prefs))],
      child: const ShopStackApp(),
    ),
  );
  await tester.pump();
  await tester.pump(const Duration(seconds: 1));
  await tester.pump(const Duration(seconds: 1));
}

Future<void> tapText(WidgetTester tester, String text) async {
  final finder = find.text(text).first;
  await tester.ensureVisible(finder);
  await tester.pump();
  await tester.tap(finder);
  await tester.pump();
  await tester.pump(const Duration(seconds: 1));
}

Future<void> goBack(WidgetTester tester) async {
  await tester.tap(find.byType(BackButton));
  await tester.pump();
  await tester.pump(const Duration(seconds: 1));
}

    expect(find.text('收货地址'), findsOneWidget);

/// 物流接口。
class LogisticsApi {
  LogisticsApi(this.client);

    return LogisticsResult.fromJson(data);
  }

  /// 按订单号查询物流轨迹。
  Future<LogisticsResult> tracksByOrder(int orderId) async {
    final data = await client.get('/logistics/tracks', query: {
      'order_id': orderId,
    });
    return LogisticsResult.fromJson(data);
  }
}

/// 站内消息接口。
class NotificationApi {
  NotificationApi(this.client);

  final ApiClient client;

  /// 消息列表。
  Future<NotificationListResult> list({
    int? type,
    bool unreadOnly = false,
    int page = 1,
    int pageSize = 20,
  }) async {
    final data = await client.get('/notifications', query: {
      'type': ?type,
      'unread_only': unreadOnly,
      'page.page': page,
      'page.page_size': pageSize,
    });
    return NotificationListResult.fromJson(data);
  }

    await client.put('/notifications/$id/read');
  }

  /// 全部标记已读。
  Future<void> markAllRead() async {
    await client.post('/notifications/read-all');
  }

  /// 未读数量。
  Future<int> unreadCount() async {
    final data = await client.get('/notifications/unread-count');
    return data['count'] is int ? data['count'] as int : 0;
  }
}

import '../models/common.dart';
import '../models/user.dart';
import 'api_client.dart';

/// 认证与用户资料相关接口。
class AuthApi {
  AuthApi(this.client);

  final ApiClient client;

  /// 账号密码登录。
  Future<AuthSession> login(String username, String password) async {
    final data = await client.post('/auth/login', body: {
      'username': username,
      'password': password,
    });
    return AuthSession.fromJson(data);
  }

  /// 注册（注册成功即返回令牌）。
  Future<AuthSession> register({
    required String username,
    required String password,
    String phone = '',
    String nickname = '',
    String smsCode = '',
  }) async {
    final data = await client.post('/auth/register', body: {
      'username': username,
      'password': password,
      'phone': phone,
      'nickname': nickname,
      'sms_code': smsCode,
    });
    return AuthSession.fromJson(data);
  }

  /// 微信登录。
  Future<AuthSession> wechatLogin({
    required String code,
    String nickname = '',
    String avatar = '',
  }) async {
    final data = await client.post('/auth/wechat', body: {
      'code': code,
      'nickname': nickname,
      'avatar': avatar,
    });
    return AuthSession.fromJson(data);
  }

  /// 当前用户资料。
  Future<User> profile() async {
    final data = await client.get('/users/me');
    return User.fromJson(data);
  }

  /// 更新用户资料。
  Future<User> updateProfile({
    String? nickname,
    String? avatar,
    String? email,
    String? gender,
    String? birthday,
  }) async {
    final data = await client.put('/users/me', body: {
      'nickname': ?nickname,
      'avatar': ?avatar,
      'email': ?email,
      'gender': ?gender,
      'birthday': ?birthday,
    });
    return User.fromJson(data);
  }

  /// 收货地址列表。
  Future<List<Address>> addresses() async {
    final data = await client.get('/users/addresses');
    return _addressList(data);
  }

  List<Address> _addressList(Map<String, dynamic> data) {
    final raw = data['addresses'];
    if (raw is List) {
      return raw
          .map((e) => Address.fromJson(
              e is Map ? e.map((k, v) => MapEntry('$k', v)) : <String, dynamic>{}))
          .toList();
    }
    return const <Address>[];
  }

  /// 新增或更新地址（带 id 为更新）。
  Future<Address> saveAddress({
    int id = 0,
    required String receiver,
    required String phone,
    required String province,
    required String city,
    required String district,
    required String detail,
    String postalCode = '',
    String tag = '',
    bool isDefault = false,
  }) async {
    final data = await client.post('/users/addresses', body: {
      'id': id,
      'receiver': receiver,
      'phone': phone,
      'province': province,
      'city': city,
      'district': district,
      'detail': detail,
      'postal_code': postalCode,
      'tag': tag,
      'is_default': isDefault,
    });
    return Address.fromJson(data);
  }

  /// 删除地址。
  Future<void> deleteAddress(int id) async {
    await client.delete('/users/addresses/$id');
  }

  /// 设为默认地址。
  Future<void> setDefaultAddress(int id) async {
    await client.put('/users/addresses/$id/default');
  }

  /// 收藏列表。
  Future<FavoritesResult> favorites({int page = 1, int pageSize = 20}) async {
    final data = await client.get('/users/favorites', query: {
      'page.page': page,
      'page.page_size': pageSize,
    });
    return FavoritesResult.fromJson(data);
  }

  /// 收藏/取消收藏。
  Future<bool> toggleFavorite(int productId) async {
    final data = await client.post('/users/favorites/toggle',
        body: {'product_id': productId});
    final value = data['favorited'];
    return value is bool ? value : false;
  }
}

=== lib/models/common.dart ===
import '../utils/json_util.dart';

/// 分页元数据，对应 shopstack.common.v1.PageMeta。
class PageMeta {
  const PageMeta({
    this.page = 1,
    this.pageSize = 20,
    this.total = 0,
    this.totalPages = 0,
  });

  final int page;
  final int pageSize;
  final int total;
  final int totalPages;

  static const PageMeta empty = PageMeta();

  factory PageMeta.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) => PageMeta(
        page: JsonUtil.intOf(json, 'page', 1),
        pageSize: JsonUtil.intOf(
            json, 'page_size', JsonUtil.intOf(json, 'pageSize', 20)),
        total: JsonUtil.intOf(json, 'total'),
        totalPages: JsonUtil.intOf(
            json, 'total_pages', JsonUtil.intOf(json, 'totalPages')),
      );

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {
        'page': page,
        'page_size': pageSize,
        'total': total,
        'total_pages': totalPages,
      };
}

/// 收货地址，对应 shopstack.common.v1.Address。
class Address {
  const Address({
    this.id = 0,
    this.receiver = '',
    this.phone = '',
    this.province = '',
    this.city = '',
    this.district = '',
    this.detail = '',
    this.postalCode = '',
    this.tag = '',
    this.isDefault = false,
  });

  final int id;
  final String receiver;
  final String phone;
  final String province;
  final String city;
  final String district;
  final String detail;
  final String postalCode;
  final String tag;
  final bool isDefault;

  String get fullAddress {
    final parts = [province, city, district].where((p) => p.isNotEmpty).toList();
    return parts.join(' ') + (detail.isEmpty ? '' : detail);
  }

  String get regionText {
    final parts = [province, city, district].where((p) => p.isNotEmpty).toList();
    return parts.join(' ');
  }

  factory Address.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) => Address(
        id: JsonUtil.intOf(json, 'id'),
        receiver: JsonUtil.stringOf(json, 'receiver'),
        phone: JsonUtil.stringOf(json, 'phone'),
        province: JsonUtil.stringOf(json, 'province'),
        city: JsonUtil.stringOf(json, 'city'),
        district: JsonUtil.stringOf(json, 'district'),
        detail: JsonUtil.stringOf(json, 'detail'),
        postalCode: JsonUtil.stringOf(
            json, 'postal_code', JsonUtil.stringOf(json, 'postalCode')),
        tag: JsonUtil.stringOf(json, 'tag'),
        isDefault: JsonUtil.boolOf(
            json, 'is_default', JsonUtil.boolOf(json, 'isDefault')),
      );

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {
        'id': id,
        'receiver': receiver,
        'phone': phone,
        'province': province,
        'city': city,
        'district': district,
        'detail': detail,
        'postal_code': postalCode,
        'tag': tag,
        'is_default': isDefault,
      };

  Address copyWith({
    int? id,
    String? receiver,
    String? phone,
    String? province,
    String? city,
    String? district,
    String? detail,
    String? postalCode,
    String? tag,
    bool? isDefault,
  }) =>
      Address(
        id: id ?? this.id,
        receiver: receiver ?? this.receiver,
        phone: phone ?? this.phone,
        province: province ?? this.province,
        city: city ?? this.city,
        district: district ?? this.district,
        detail: detail ?? this.detail,
        postalCode: postalCode ?? this.postalCode,
        tag: tag ?? this.tag,
        isDefault: isDefault ?? this.isDefault,
      );
}

/// 图片项，对应 shopstack.common.v1.ImageItem。
class ImageItem {
  const ImageItem({this.url = '', this.alt = '', this.sort = 0});

  final String url;
  final String alt;
  final int sort;

  factory ImageItem.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) => ImageItem(
        url: JsonUtil.stringOf(json, 'url'),
        alt: JsonUtil.stringOf(json, 'alt'),
        sort: JsonUtil.intOf(json, 'sort'),
      );

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {'url': url, 'alt': alt, 'sort': sort};
}

/// 通用操作结果，对应 shopstack.common.v1.OperationResult。
class OperationResult {
  const OperationResult({this.success = false, this.message = ''});

  final bool success;
  final String message;

  factory OperationResult.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) => OperationResult(
        success: JsonUtil.boolOf(json, 'success'),
        message: JsonUtil.stringOf(json, 'message'),
      );
}

/// 分页响应包装：各接口数组字段名不同，统一收敛为 items + meta。
class PagedResult<T> {
  const PagedResult({required this.items, this.meta = PageMeta.empty});

  final List<T> items;
  final PageMeta meta;

  bool get isEmpty => items.isEmpty;
  int get total => meta.total;
}

/// 列表响应包装（无分页）。
class ListResult<T> {
  const ListResult(this.items);

  final List<T> items;

  bool get isEmpty => items.isEmpty;
}

import '../utils/enums.dart';
import '../utils/json_util.dart';
import 'common.dart';

/// 商品分类，对应 shopstack.product.v1.Category。
class Category {
  const Category({
    this.id = 0,
    this.name = '',
    this.parentId = 0,
    this.icon = '',
    this.description = '',
    this.sort = 0,
    this.visible = true,
    this.productCount = 0,
    this.children = const <Category>[],
  });

  final int id;
  final String name;
  final int parentId;
  final String icon;
  final String description;
  final int sort;
  final bool visible;
  final int productCount;
  final List<Category> children;

  factory Category.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) => Category(
        id: JsonUtil.intOf(json, 'id'),
        name: JsonUtil.stringOf(json, 'name'),
        parentId: JsonUtil.intOf(json, 'parent_id',
            JsonUtil.intOf(json, 'parentId')),
        icon: JsonUtil.stringOf(json, 'icon'),
        description: JsonUtil.stringOf(json, 'description'),
        sort: JsonUtil.intOf(json, 'sort'),
        visible: JsonUtil.boolOf(json, 'visible', true),
        productCount: JsonUtil.intOf(json, 'product_count',
            JsonUtil.intOf(json, 'productCount')),
        children: JsonUtil.asMapList(json['children'])
            .map(Category.fromJson)
            .toList(),
      );

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {
        'id': id,
        'name': name,
        'parent_id': parentId,
        'icon': icon,
        'description': description,
        'sort': sort,
        'visible': visible,
        'product_count': productCount,
        'children': children.map((c) => c.toJson()).toList(),
      };
}

/// 商品，对应 shopstack.product.v1.Product。
class Product {
  const Product({
    this.id = 0,
    this.title = '',
    this.subtitle = '',
    this.cover = '',
    this.categoryId = 0,
    this.categoryName = '',
    this.minPriceCents = 0,
    this.maxPriceCents = 0,
    this.salesCount = 0,
    this.stockTotal = 0,
    this.status = ProductStatus.onShelf,
    this.description = '',
    this.tags = const <String>[],
    this.createdAt = '',
    this.updatedAt = '',
  });

  final int id;
  final String title;
  final String subtitle;
  final String cover;
  final int categoryId;
  final String categoryName;
  final int minPriceCents;
  final int maxPriceCents;
  final int salesCount;
  final int stockTotal;
  final ProductStatus status;
  final String description;
  final List<String> tags;
  final String createdAt;
  final String updatedAt;

  bool get onShelf => status == ProductStatus.onShelf;
  bool get hasDiscount => maxPriceCents > minPriceCents && maxPriceCents > 0;

  factory Product.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) => Product(
        id: JsonUtil.intOf(json, 'id'),
        title: JsonUtil.stringOf(json, 'title'),
        subtitle: JsonUtil.stringOf(json, 'subtitle'),
        cover: JsonUtil.stringOf(json, 'cover'),

        salesCount: JsonUtil.intOf(json, 'sales_count',

        stockTotal: JsonUtil.intOf(json, 'stock_total',

        status: parseEnumValue<ProductStatus>(

          ProductStatus.values,
          (v) => v.wire,

        ),
        description: JsonUtil.stringOf(json, 'description'),
        tags: JsonUtil.asStringList(json['tags']),
        createdAt: JsonUtil.stringOf(json, 'created_at',
            JsonUtil.stringOf(json, 'createdAt')),
        updatedAt: JsonUtil.stringOf(json, 'updated_at',
            JsonUtil.stringOf(json, 'updatedAt')),
      );

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {
        'id': id,
        'title': title,
        'subtitle': subtitle,
        'cover': cover,
        'category_id': categoryId,
        'category_name': categoryName,
        'min_price_cents': minPriceCents,
        'max_price_cents': maxPriceCents,
        'sales_count': salesCount,
        'stock_total': stockTotal,
        'status': status.wire,
        'description': description,
        'tags': tags,
        'created_at': createdAt,
        'updated_at': updatedAt,
      };
}

/// 用户优惠券，对应 shopstack.marketing.v1.UserCoupon。
class UserCoupon {
  const UserCoupon({
    this.id = 0,
    this.userId = 0,
    this.couponId = 0,
    this.coupon,
    this.status = UserCouponStatus.unused,
    this.orderId = 0,
    this.receivedAt = '',
    this.usedAt = '',
  });

  final int id;
  final int userId;
  final int couponId;
  final Coupon? coupon;
  final UserCouponStatus status;
  final int orderId;
  final String receivedAt;
  final String usedAt;

  String get name => coupon?.name ?? '';
  CouponType get type => coupon?.type ?? CouponType.unspecified;
  int get faceValueCents => coupon?.faceValueCents ?? 0;
  int get percent => coupon?.percent ?? 100;
  int get minAmountCents => coupon?.minAmountCents ?? 0;
  int get maxDiscountCents => coupon?.maxDiscountCents ?? 0;
  String get code => coupon?.code ?? '';

  /// 优惠金额说明文案。
  String get discountText {
    switch (type) {
      case CouponType.fixed:
        return '减 ${(faceValueCents / 100).toStringAsFixed(0)} 元';
      case CouponType.percent:
        return '${(percent / 10).toStringAsFixed(1)} 折';
      case CouponType.shipping:
        return '免运费';
      case CouponType.unspecified:
        return '优惠券';
    }
  }

  factory UserCoupon.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) => UserCoupon(
        id: JsonUtil.intOf(json, 'id'),
        userId: JsonUtil.intOf(json, 'user_id', JsonUtil.intOf(json, 'userId')),
        couponId: JsonUtil.intOf(json, 'coupon_id',
            JsonUtil.intOf(json, 'couponId')),
        coupon: json['coupon'] == null
            ? null
            : Coupon.fromJson(JsonUtil.asMap(json['coupon'])),
        status: parseEnumValue<UserCouponStatus>(
          json['status'],
          UserCouponStatus.values,
          (v) => v.wire,
          (v) => enumKey(v.name),
        ),
        orderId:
            JsonUtil.intOf(json, 'order_id', JsonUtil.intOf(json, 'orderId')),
        receivedAt: JsonUtil.stringOf(json, 'received_at',
            JsonUtil.stringOf(json, 'receivedAt')),
        usedAt:
            JsonUtil.stringOf(json, 'used_at', JsonUtil.stringOf(json, 'usedAt')),
      );
}

Let's do it.

Script:
```bash
cd apps/flutter/lib/pages
for f in *_page.dart; do
  name="${f%_page.dart}"
  mkdir -p "$name"
  sed "s|import '\.\./|import '../../|g" "$f" > "$name/$f"
  rm "$f"
done
```

1. `lib/models/expansion.dart` — models for new entities.
2. `lib/api/expansion_api.dart`? No — plan says add methods to corresponding domain files. I'll:
   - `lib/api/product_api.dart` += channels, channelProducts, productParams, productQuestions, filteredReviews, reviewTags, myReviews
   - `lib/api/marketing_api.dart` += signIn, signInCalendar, tasks, taskDetail, promotionSessions, groupBuys, groupBuyDetail, joinGroupBuy, myGroupBuys
   - `lib/api/order_api.dart` += afterSales, afterSaleDetail, createAfterSale, refundReasons, confirmOrder (preview)
   - `lib/api/logistics_api.dart` += tracks (already), trackingByNo, freight, companies, ranges
   - `lib/api/notification_api.dart` += categories
   - `lib/api/payment_api.dart` += rechargeConfig, recharge, recharges, balanceLogs, payChannels
   - `lib/api/user_api.dart` (new) — level, growthLogs, invoiceTemplates, notificationSettings, security, realname, feedback, bindPhone
   - `lib/api/content_api.dart` (new) — helpArticles, helpArticle, regions

3. `lib/mock/expansion_mock.dart` — mock data.
4. `lib/providers/expansion_providers.dart` — providers with fallback.
5. Shared views in `lib/widgets/`.
6. 56 pages.
7. Routes.

Let me design the models. I'll create one file `lib/models/expansion.dart` with all new models:

- `Channel` (id, name, code, description, icon, sort, visible, productCount)
- `ChannelType` enum-ish: I'll use string channel types ('recommend','newest',...).
- `ProductParam` (id, productId, name, value, sort)
- `ProductQuestion` (id, productId, userNickname, question, answer, answered, createdAt, answeredAt)
- `ReviewTag` (id, name, count)
- `SignInInfo` (signedToday, continuousDays, totalDays, rewardPoints, calendar list)
- `SignInCalendarDay` (date, signed, rewardPoints)
- `MarketingTask` (id, title, description, type, target, progress, completed, rewardPoints, rewardCents, status)
- `PromotionSession` (id, promotionId, name, startTime, endTime, status, discountCents, percent)
- `GroupBuy` (id, title, productId, productTitle, cover, priceCents, originalPriceCents, requiredMembers, joinedMembers, startTime, endTime, status, perUserLimit)
- `GroupBuyMember` (id, groupBuyId, nickname, avatar, joinedAt, isLeader)
- `AfterSale` (id, orderId, orderNo, type, status, amountCents, reason, description, createdAt, images, refundNo)
- `RefundReason` (id, name, description, required)
- `LogisticsCompany` (id, name, code, phone, logo)
- `DeliveryRange` (id, name, company, provinces, freeShipping, freightCents, remark)
- `FreightQuote` (freightCents, company, estimatedDays, remark)
- `UserLevel` (id, name, level, growthValue, minGrowth, maxGrowth, discountPercent, benefits)
- `GrowthLog` (id, source, amount, balance, remark, createdAt)
- `InvoiceTemplate` (id, title, taxNo, type, email, isDefault, createdAt)
- `NotificationSetting` (id, category, name, enabled, description)
- `SecurityInfo` (passwordSet, phoneBound, emailBound, wechatBound, realnameVerified, lastLoginAt, loginDevices)
- `HelpArticle` (id, title, category, summary, content, updatedAt)
- `Region` (id, name, parentId, level, code)
- `RechargeConfig` (minAmountCents, maxAmountCents, bonusRules, channels)
- `RechargeRecord` (id, rechargeNo, amountCents, bonusCents, channel, status, createdAt, paidAt)
- `BalanceLog` (id, type, amountCents, balanceCents, remark, createdAt)
- `PayChannel` (id, name, code, enabled, icon, description)
- `NotificationCategory` (id, name, code, icon, unreadCount)

That's a lot but manageable.

To keep this efficient, I'll use a generic approach for list responses.

Let me write the models file now. I'll use `JsonUtil` helpers.

Important: mock data must use camelCase in Dart (since interceptor only transforms network layer). The models' `fromJson` uses `JsonUtil.intOf(json, 'page_size', JsonUtil.intOf(json,'pageSize'))` pattern for snake/camel tolerance. I'll follow that pattern so both work.

product_api ok

  /// 我发表过的评价。
  Future<(List<Review>, PageMeta)> myReviews({
    int page = 1,
    int pageSize = 20,
  }) async {
    final data = await client.get('/users/reviews', query: {
      'page.page': page,
      'page.page_size': pageSize,
    });
    final list = JsonUtil.asMapList(data['reviews']).map(Review.fromJson).toList();
    return (list, PageMeta.fromJson(JsonUtil.asMap(data['meta'])));
  }

  /// 评价标签。
  Future<List<ReviewTag>> reviewTags({int? productId}) async {
    final data = await client.get('/reviews/tags', query: {'product_id': ?productId});
    return JsonUtil.asMapList(data['tags']).map(ReviewTag.fromJson).toList();
  }
}

## 🤖 MiniMax-M3.1-Flash · 2026-10-20 14:45:42

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

````
`sku ??= detail.skus.first` — so skuId 0 falls back to the first sku. So `addToCart(productId: id, skuId: 0, quantity: 1)` works for mock. For the real API, skuId 0 would be invalid, but the real API path requires a valid sku anyway. To be safe, I'll use the product detail provider to get the first sku id.

Simplest robust approach: use `ref.read(productDetailProvider(productId).future)` to get skus. But that's async and could fail.

Alternative: don't offer "add to cart" on the channel grid; just navigate to the product detail. That avoids the dead/incorrect action. But an add-to-cart button is nice UX. I'll implement it with a lookup of the first sku via MockData.skus map + detail provider.

Let me use:
```dart
final detail = ref.read(productDetailProvider(productId));
final skuId = detail.asData?.value.skus.firstOrNull?.id ?? 0;
```
Need `firstOrNull` from package:collection — not available. Use manual:
```dart
final skus = detail.asData?.value.skus ?? const <ProductSku>[];
final skuId = skus.isEmpty ? 0 : skus.first.id;
```

That's clean. productDetailProvider is in catalog_provider. Let me update the channel widget.
